Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CD-LAM，通过因果去偏的潜在动作学习提升动作条件世界模型的可控性与适应效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界模型需要大量带动作标签的数据，而真实机器人数据采集昂贵；潜在动作模型可从无标签视频中推断动作，但现有重建式训练容易把背景、未接触物体等与动作无关的视觉因素编码进潜在动作，削弱模型可控性。

Method: 作者提出 CD-LAM 框架，包含三类高效微调目标：以具身主体为中心的重建、以动作为中心的对比学习，以及潜在空间校准；同时提出评估潜在动作偏差、动作跟随能力和鲁棒性的指标。

Result: 在 2B 和 14B 参数规模的 ACWM 骨干上，CD-LAM 显著提升潜在动作可控性、机器人动作跟随、视觉保真度和适应效率；仅需 6k 微调步，并比基线减少超过 12 倍的机器人动作适配更新。

Conclusion: 动作无关偏差是限制 LAM 驱动 ACWM 可控性的关键问题；通过因果去偏和校准潜在动作表示，CD-LAM 能更有效地学习动作相关动态，并提高世界模型在机器人任务中的实用性。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models (ACWMs) aim to simulate future observations conditioned on embodied actions, offering a promising foundation for robot planning, policy evaluation, and data augmentation. However, learning controllable ACWMs requires large-scale action-labeled data, which remains costly to collect in the real world. Latent action models (LAMs) mitigate this bottleneck by inferring latent actions from unlabeled videos, but existing LAMs are typically trained with reconstruction-only objectives and therefore entangle action-relevant dynamics with action-irrelevant visual factors such as backgrounds and untouched objects. In this work, we identify this action-irrelevant bias as a key obstacle to controllable ACWMs and introduce evaluation metrics to measure latent-action bias, action following, and robustness. We propose CD-LAM, a causally debiased framework for LAM-based ACWMs. CD-LAM introduces three efficient fine-tuning objectives: embodiment-centric reconstruction, action-centric contrastive learning, and latent space calibration, which together encourage embodiment-focused, action-aware, and calibrated non-collapsed latent action representations. Experiments on 2B and 14B ACWM backbones show that CD-LAM substantially improves latent-action controllability, downstream robot-action following, visual fidelity, and adaptation efficiency, requiring only 6k fine-tuning steps and more than 12$\times$ fewer robot-action adaptation updates than the baseline.

</details>


### [36] [HiHR: Hierarchical Hyperbolic Representation for Aerial-Ground Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09186)
*Qiwei Yang,Pingping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 HiHR 框架，用于空地行人重识别，通过文本引导的多粒度特征融合与双层级双曲空间学习，同时建模跨视角一致性和视角特异判别线索，在四个基准上取得有效结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AG-ReID 方法通常直接对齐空中视角与地面视角的特征，容易忽略不同视角下独有且有判别力的信息，导致跨平台检索性能受限。作者希望在保持身份跨视角一致性的同时，保留视角特异线索。

Method: 方法包括三部分：首先基于预训练视觉-文本编码器提取多粒度视觉特征；其次提出 Text-guided Multi-granularity Fusion（TMF），利用文本引导融合多粒度特征以增强身份表征；最后提出 Hierarchical Hyperbolic Learning（HHL），在双曲空间中构建层级特征结构：粗粒度层保证身份可分性与跨视角一致性，细粒度层保留视角特异的判别信息。

Result: 在四个 AG-ReID 基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该框架能够有效聚合视角不变特征与视角特异特征，并优于或验证了相对现有方法的有效性。

Conclusion: HiHR 通过层级化双曲表征和文本引导的多粒度融合，缓解了空地视角差异带来的特征对齐问题，为 AG-ReID 提供了一种兼顾跨视角一致性与视角特异判别性的有效方案。

Abstract: Aerial-Ground Person Re-IDentification (AG-ReID) aims to retrieve the same person across heterogeneous aerial and ground camera platforms. Although great progress has been made, existing methods remain suboptimal due to the direct feature alignment across views, overlooking view-specific cues. To address this issue, we propose a novel Hierarchical Hyperbolic Representation (HiHR) framework for AG-ReID. More specifically, we first extract multi-granularity features based on pre-trained visual-text encoders. Then, we propose a Text-guided Multi-granularity Fusion (TMF) to fuse multi-granularity features and enhance the representation ability of identity features. Furthermore, we introduce the Hierarchical Hyperbolic Learning (HHL) to construct a hierarchical feature structure in a hyperbolic space. This hierarchy includes a coarse level that ensures identity separability and cross-view consistency, and a fine level that preserves view-specific discriminative cues. As a result, our proposed framework can effectively aggregate view-invariant and view-specific discriminative features for AG-ReID. Extensive experiments on four AG-ReID benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework. The source code is available at https://github.com/YangQiWei3/HiHR.

</details>


### [37] [YeTI: You Only Need Two Noisy Images for Real-World sRGB Noise Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09193)
*Jaekyun Ko,Byung Wan Lim,Soomin Lee,Dongjin Kim,Tae Hyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YeTI 是一种面向真实 sRGB 图像去噪的噪声生成框架，仅利用同一场景的两张噪声观测学习真实噪声分布，可在无干净图像和无相机元数据条件下合成逼真的信号相关噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实 sRGB 图像噪声具有非线性、信号相关等复杂特性，且获取严格对齐的干净/噪声图像对困难；监督式去噪器容易受限于小规模配对数据并过拟合，自监督方法也依赖足够多样的噪声观测。因此需要一种可扩展的真实噪声合成方法，能够在无需干净真值和相机元数据的情况下建模真实噪声。

Method: 论文提出 YeTI 框架：首先使用 Reconstruction Autoencoder 解耦图像中的场景结构与噪声特征；随后采用一步式 Conditional Diffusion Transformer，并通过一致性目标训练来建模潜在噪声分布。推理时，给定单张噪声图像，模型可在保持场景内容不变的同时生成真实且信号相关的噪声。

Result: 实验在多个真实图像去噪与噪声建模基准上验证了有效性：在 SIDD 上评估噪声生成质量，并在 SIDD+、MAI2021、SID 等涵盖手机与多类消费级相机传感器的数据集上测试泛化能力；此外，在 DND 下游去噪任务中，使用 YeTI 合成图像训练的去噪器取得了较强的真实场景性能。

Conclusion: YeTI 展示了在无干净图像、无相机元数据条件下进行真实 sRGB 噪声生成的可行性与实用价值，可为真实图像去噪提供更可扩展的数据合成方案，并提升下游去噪模型的真实场景泛化能力。

Abstract: Real-world sRGB image denoising remains challenging due to the nonlinear characteristics of sensor noise and the difficulty of acquiring aligned clean-noisy image pairs. Supervised denoisers often overfit to limited paired datasets, while self-supervised methods still depend on sufficiently diverse noisy observations. These limitations motivate scalable noise synthesis methods that can model real-world noise without clean ground truth or camera metadata. We propose YeTI, a real-world sRGB noise generation framework that learns from only two noisy observations of the same scene. YeTI uses a Reconstruction Autoencoder to disentangle scene structure and noise characteristics, and models the latent noise distribution with a one-step Conditional Diffusion Transformer trained using consistency objectives. Given a single noisy input at inference time, YeTI generates realistic, signal-dependent noise while preserving the underlying scene content. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of YeTI across real-world benchmarks. We evaluate noise generation on SIDD and further assess generalization on SIDD+, MAI2021, and SID, covering smartphone and diverse consumer-camera sensors. Downstream denoising results on DND further show that denoisers trained with YeTI-synthesized images achieve strong real-world performance, highlighting the practical value of clean-image-free and metadata-free noise generation.

</details>


### [38] [Glob3R: Global Structure-from-Motion with 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09225)
*Junyuan Deng,Heng Li,Kejie Qiu,Lingteng Qiu,Rui Peng,Weichao Shen,Weihao Yuan,Siyu Zhu,Zilong Dong,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Glob3R 将3D几何基础模型的前馈预测与全局SfM优化结合，通过密集匹配生成可靠多视图轨迹，并用滑动窗口关联、运动平均和束调整提升长序列与大规模无序图像集的重建精度与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D几何基础模型可直接从图像预测相机位姿和3D点，但精度仍有限；面对长序列或大规模无序图像集时常需分块处理，容易产生漂移、尺度不一致和全局不一致问题。因此需要一种既保留基础模型鲁棒先验，又具备全局几何优化能力的可扩展重建框架。

Method: 方法以冻结的Pi3X骨干为基础，增加轻量级密集匹配头，在参考帧与邻近视图之间预测图像warp；再将密集warp转换为稀疏但可靠的多视图特征轨迹，用作全局优化的对应约束。系统采用基于关键帧的滑动窗口关联策略，在重叠窗口间传播轨迹和相对位姿；最后通过全局运动平均和束调整细化相机位姿、降低尺度不一致，并恢复稠密场景几何。

Result: 在室内、室外、大规模驾驶场景和无序SfM基准上的实验表明，Glob3R比前馈式基础模型基线和近期可扩展重建方法更准确、更鲁棒；相较经典SfM流程也表现出更强鲁棒性。优化后的位姿还能提升神经渲染质量。

Conclusion: Glob3R证明了将3D基础模型的先验能力与传统全局几何优化相结合，可以有效解决长序列和大规模图像集重建中的漂移、尺度不一致与精度不足问题，是一种兼具鲁棒性、准确性和可扩展性的全局三维重建方案。

Abstract: Recent 3D geometric foundation models, such as VGGT, provide robust feed-forward 3D reconstruction by directly predicting camera poses and 3D scene points from input images. However, their results remain inaccurate, and scaling them to long sequences or large unordered image sets typically requires chunk-wise processing, which can introduce drift and inconsistency. We present Glob3R, a global SfM-style reconstruction built on 3D foundation models. Our key idea is to explicitly optimize feed-forward geometric predictions. To this end, we augment a frozen Pi3X backbone with a lightweight dense matching head that predicts image warps between selected reference frames and neighboring views. These dense warps are converted into sparse but reliable multi-view feature tracks, which provide correspondence constraints for global optimization. We further introduce a keyframe-based sliding-window association strategy that propagates tracks and relative poses across overlapping windows, enabling scalable reconstruction. Finally, we perform global motion averaging and bundle adjustment to refine camera poses, reduce scale inconsistencies, and recover dense scene geometry. Extensive experiments on indoor, outdoor, large-scale driving, and unordered SfM benchmarks demonstrate that Glob3R achieves robust and accurate reconstruction. It consistently improves over feed-forward foundation-model baselines and recent scalable reconstruction methods, while being more robust than classical SfM pipelines. The refined poses also lead to higher-quality neural rendering, validating the benefit of combining foundation-model priors with global geometric optimization. Project page: https://junyuandeng.github.io/Glob3r

</details>


### [39] [AnythingReality: Robust Online Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Open-Vocabulary VR Scene Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09260)
*Timofei Kozlov,Dmitrii Maliukov,Andrey Marchenko,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向真实噪声数据的在线3D Gaussian Splatting集成系统，支持实时VR探索与语音驱动的视觉语言模型交互，并在重建质量和交互识别方面取得较好结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线3D Gaussian Splatting方法通常依赖干净深度数据或外部位姿，难以直接适应噪声较大的真实世界输入；同时，在线三维重建结果的沉浸式探索和自然语言/语音交互能力仍不充分。

Method: 系统将ORB-SLAM3位姿估计与在线Gaussian重建结合，用于处理真实场景中的噪声数据；同时构建VR管线以支持对增量重建结果的实时沉浸式浏览，并加入语义模块，将语音命令转写为文本，生成场景描述并记录兴趣点。

Result: 相较于先进在线Gaussian Splatting方法，本文在自建数据集上提升了14.5% PSNR、8.6% SSIM，并降低14.3% LPIPS；在TUM-RGBD上提升了11.7% PSNR、7.8% SSIM，并降低21.6% LPIPS。系统在不同质量—速度配置下达到相当或更优帧率，VLM目标识别率为88%。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一个可用于真实场景的端到端在线三维重建与交互系统，在鲁棒位姿估计、实时VR可视化和语义交互之间实现了有效集成，提升了在线重建质量与用户交互能力。

Abstract: We present a novel integrated architecture for robust online 3D Gaussian splatting, real-time VR exploration, and speech-driven Vision-Language-Model interaction. Unlike methods assuming clean depth or external poses, our system combines ORB-SLAM3-based pose estimation with online Gaussian reconstruction for noisy real-world data. A VR pipeline enables immersive exploration of incremental reconstructions; a semantic module transcribes voice commands, generates scene descriptions, and records points of interest. We achieve an 88% VLM object-recognition rate.

TL;DR: 本文提出 SAN，通过基于视觉混淆性的硬负样本挖掘改进手语检索，显著提升细粒度检索性能并保持粗粒度准确率。

Abstract: Sign Language Retrieval (SLRet) enables efficient access to sign language content but remains fragile in fine-grained scenarios where visually similar signs must be distinguished. We show that this limitation does not stem from model capacity, but from ineffective hard negative supervision. To address this issue, we propose Sign-Aware Hard Negative Mining (SAN), which constructs hard negatives based on visual confusability in the sign embedding space rather than linguistic similarity. Experiments on PHOENIX-2014T demonstrate that SAN substantially improves fine-grained retrieval performance while preserving coarse-grained accuracy, highlighting the importance of aligning negative supervision with visual ambiguity in sign language retrieval.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内机器人需要在长时间间隔、显著视角变化和强光照变化后重新识别曾经见过的物体。现有多目标跟踪主要适合视频帧率下的短期关联；行人/车辆重识别方法缺少持久记忆机制；视频目标分割方法更多依赖反应式干扰过滤，难以保证全局身份一致性。因此需要一种面向通用室内物体的长时身份保持与重识别方法。

Conclusion: REMIND 表明，在不依赖几何位姿或深度的条件下，结合长期外观记忆、多层次视觉描述、空间上下文和歧义控制，可以有效解决室内通用物体的长时多目标重识别问题。其公开系统、评估框架和数据集也有助于推动该方向的可复现研究。

Abstract: Mobile robots operating indoors must re-identify previously observed objects after long temporal gaps, significant viewpoint changes, and severe illumination variations. This remains a challenging problem: multi-object tracking methods are optimized for short-term association of pedestrians and vehicles at video rates, person and vehicle re-identification approaches lack persistent memory mechanisms, and state-of-the-art video object segmentation techniques rely on reactive distractor filtering rather than enforcing global identity consistency.
  To address these limitations, we present REMIND, an online tracker designed for long-term multi-object re-identification of generic indoor objects from monocular RGB imagery, requiring neither camera pose nor depth. Motivated by evidence from visual cognition that humans rely on accumulated appearance familiarity and spatial context rather than explicit self-localization, REMIND combines frozen DINOv3 features with a dual-bank multi-prototype appearance memory, part- and background-level descriptors, a neighbour-context reasoning module exploiting spatial co-occurrence, and joint Hungarian assignment with ambiguity-aware safeguards. On a purpose-built indoor dataset featuring controlled revisits and dense same-class clutter, REMIND reaches 90.35% IDF1, nearly 20 points above a state-of-the-art video object segmentation baseline and more than 36 above a strong tracking-by-detection baseline. On ScanNet++, it attains the highest IDF1 in every setting but one, end-to-end detection over all scenes, where the tracking-by-detection baseline is marginally ahead while REMIND still associates and recovers identities more accurately; it also completes every scene, whereas the video object segmentation baseline exhausts GPU memory on 66.9% under YOLO detections. The complete system, evaluation framework, and dataset are publicly released.

</details>

*Libo Lin,Shuangli Du,Minghua Zhao,Zhenzhen You,Shun Lv,Yiguang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种更稳健的单目深度嵌入立体匹配框架，通过降低分支耦合、引入软约束，并在特征提取与GRU迭代中融合单目几何信息，实现更好的泛化性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目方法具备丰富上下文先验但几何精度不足；立体方法几何准确但在弱纹理和遮挡区域表现较差。已有融合方法常依赖不稳定的单目-立体对齐，且错误单目线索可能损害匹配性能，因此需要更可靠、容错性更强的单目深度嵌入机制。

Method: 作者重新设计单目深度嵌入策略：通过减少分支耦合避免捷径学习；使用来自单目深度的软约束替代硬约束以增强对单目误差的容忍度；将单目深度图与RGB图像融合，用于增强深度边界感知并抑制匹配歧义；在特征提取阶段使上下文特征编码全局几何信息；利用单目深度梯度特征引导GRU中的视差迭代更新；同时提出边缘置信度估计与边缘感知损失，以缓解数据增强导致的监督视差边界模糊问题。

Result: 该方法在多个标准立体匹配基准上达到SOTA性能，表现出较强的跨数据泛化能力，并同时提升了匹配精度。

Conclusion: 论文表明，单目深度信息若以低耦合、软约束和梯度引导的方式嵌入立体匹配网络，可有效结合单目上下文先验与双目几何优势，减少错误单目线索的负面影响，并提升复杂区域的匹配鲁棒性。

Abstract: Generally, monocular methods capture rich contextual priors but lack geometric precision, whereas stereo methods are geometrically accurate yet struggle in textureless and occluded regions. Several approaches attempt to combine their strengths to enhance the generalization of stereo matching (SM) by aligning monocular depth with stereo information. However, establishing a stable and generalizable alignment is challenging, and unreliable monocular cues can substantially degrade performance. This paper rethinks monocular depth embedding. First, to prevent shortcut learning, we reduce branch coupling instead of expanding network width. Second, we construct soft constraints instead of hard ones from monocular depth to improve tolerance to monocular depth errors. Based on the principles, we integrate monocular information into both feature extraction and GRU iterations. Specifically, the monocular depth map is fused with the RGB image to sharpen depth boundary perception and suppress matching ambiguities. The fused image is then used for feature extraction, allowing the contextual features to encode global geometric information. Furthermore, the monocular depth gradient feature is employed to guide disparity updates, helping to escape local oscillations. Finally, to address the boundary blurring of supervised disparity caused by data augmentation, we propose an edge confidence estimation method and an edge-aware loss function. Our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on multiple standard benchmarks, demonstrating excellent generalization while improving accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/linliboabc-maker/stereo-matching-digital.

</details>

*Anguelos Nicolaou,Antonella Ambrosio,Desiree Di Donato,Georg Vogeler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TextileNet：仅用合成数据训练的全卷积多任务网络，生成像素级纹理嵌入，并零样本迁移到历史手稿分析；同时建立人类基线评测，验证其在书写者检索中的潜力，并提示基于笔迹推断性别需谨慎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 档案古文字学中的自动书写者识别受限于标注训练数据稀缺、开放书写者集合以及历史图像退化等问题；此外，相关任务缺乏可靠的人类表现基线，特别是对中世纪晚期文本书写风格辨别能力的定量参照。

Result: 实验表明 TextileNet 嵌入在古文字学场景中具有一定可信度，可用于零样本检索和书写风格相关分析；人类测验提供了 late medieval text 脚本风格辨别的人类基线。结果同时显示，用笔迹进行性别相关判断存在不确定性和风险。

Conclusion: TextileNet 展示了合成数据训练与零样本迁移在历史手稿书写者分析中的可行性；论文的评测贡献在于引入人类基线。但关于性别识别的结论应保守解读，需避免将笔迹特征与性别作过度确定性关联。

Abstract: Automatic writer identification systems have progressed remarkably in recent years, yet their deployment in archival paleography remains limited by the scarcity of labeled training data, open scribe sets, and degraded image quality. We present TextileNet, a fully convolutional multi-task network trained exclusively on synthetic data to produce dense pixel-level texture embeddings, which we transfer zeroshot to historical manuscript analysis. As an original contribution to evaluation methodology, we designed a paleographic visual quiz of 80 pair and triplet questions and administered it to a range from lay participants to senior paleographers under strict anonymity, establishing to our knowledge for the first time a human baseline for script-style discrimination on late medieval text. We employ TextileNet embeddings to perform zero-shot retrieval on sub-word granularity for hand and gender identification. Our experimental results help in building the credibility of TextileNet in the paleographic domain, but more than that demonstrate in experimental terms that the question of gender in handwriting needs to be treated with caution.

</details>

*Isarun Chamveha,Tretap Promwiset,Napat Wanchaitanawong,Trongtum Tongdee,Pairash Saiviroonporn,Warasinee Chaisangmongkon*

TL;DR: 本文介绍并验证了 Inspectra CXR v5：一个面向泰国胸片数据本地化开发的深度学习系统，可同时进行多标签胸部疾病分类与弱监督病灶定位，并在院内与多中心外部数据上取得高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部X线仍是最常用的胸部影像检查，但泰国及东南亚放射科医师短缺，限制了及时、准确判读；同时既有模型若未适配本地人群和医疗场景，性能可能下降，因此需要基于泰国本地数据开发并验证可靠模型。

Result: 在19,871例院内放射科医师验证测试集上，9类重要临床病变的平均AUROC为0.994，平均敏感性92.4%、特异性98.6%。在5,992例来自泰国13家医院的外部泛化集上，平均AUROC为0.970。定位方面，在4,549例标注病例上，平均病灶定位分数LLF为77.9%，每图非病灶定位0.59次。可用性评估中，分类一致性93.6%，定位一致性94.7%，平均SUS为89。

Conclusion: Inspectra CXR v5显示出高分类准确性、较强跨医院泛化能力和较好的病灶定位能力；本地化开发的胸片AI系统有潜力缓解放射科资源不足，并获得临床医师较高信任度。

Abstract: Chest radiography (CXR) remains the most widely used thoracic imaging modality, yet expert interpretation is constrained by a severe shortage of radiologists in Thailand and across Southeast Asia. Local adaptation of deep learning models to Thai data has been shown to substantially improve accuracy on Thai populations. Here we present the development and comprehensive validation of the chest radiograph analysis model in Inspectra CXR version 5, a deep learning system that performs multi-label thoracic disease classification and weakly supervised lesion localization within a single model. The architecture couples a DenseNet-121 backbone with Attend-and-Compare Modules (ACM) and a Probabilistic Class Activation Map (PCAM) aggregation layer, producing a per-condition classification score and heatmap simultaneously. The model was developed on 874,858 frontal chest radiographs with paired radiologist reports from Siriraj Hospital, Bangkok. On a held-out, radiologist-verified in-domain test set of 19,871 cases, it achieved a mean AUROC of 0.994 (mean sensitivity 92.4%, specificity 98.6%) across nine clinically important conditions. On an independent generalization set of 5,992 cases from 13 hospitals across Thailand, the mean AUROC was 0.970, indicating robust transfer across sites. For localization, evaluated on 4,549 radiologist-annotated cases, the model attained a mean lesion-localization fraction (LLF) of 77.9% at 0.59 non-lesion localizations per image. In a usability evaluation with five thoracic radiologists, the system reached a classification concordance of 93.6%, a localization concordance of 94.7%, and a mean System Usability Scale (SUS) score of 89. These results indicate that a locally developed, localization-capable CXR system can deliver high accuracy, generalize across heterogeneous Thai hospitals, and earn the trust of practicing radiologists.

</details>

*Raza Yunus,Benjamin Ummenhofer,Jan Eric Lenssen,Eddy Ilg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向刚性运动物体的可重光照逆渲染流程，证明物体运动带来的多样光照-表面交互可缓解材质与光照分解的歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆渲染需要将观测到的表面出射辐射分解为材质与照明，以支持重光照和增强现实等应用；但该问题高度病态，不同材质-光照组合可能产生相同颜色。传统上多光照采集有助于约束优化，本文关注刚性运动物体在单段视频中产生的多样观察是否也能提供类似约束。

Method: 作者提出一个可重光照框架，将物体跟踪、三维重建与逆渲染结合起来，用于一般刚性运动物体。该方法利用物体刚性运动过程中不同姿态导致的多样光照-表面交互来联合估计几何、材质和照明，从而缓解材质-光照歧义。

Result: 在合成数据上的实验表明，相比静态物体，处于刚性运动中的物体能显著提升重建材质的准确性。真实手持物体的RGB视频实验也显示，即使存在现实噪声，该流程仍能保留运动带来的材质分解优势。

Conclusion: 刚性运动不仅是重建中的挑战，也可作为逆渲染中的有用信号；通过结合跟踪、重建与逆渲染，运动物体的观测可有效帮助解耦材质与照明，并提升可重光照结果的可靠性。

Abstract: Decomposing outgoing surface radiance into material and illumination during inverse rendering is essential for applications such as relighting and augmented reality, yet it is severely ill-posed since multiple combinations can result in the same observed colour. Capturing an object under multiple lighting conditions usually helps resolve this ambiguity as it constrains the optimization towards correct solutions. In this work, we explore the potential of reconstructing rigidly moving objects -- which provides observations of diverse light-surface interactions -- to resolve the material-lighting ambiguity in inverse rendering. For this purpose, we introduce a relightable approach that marries object tracking and reconstruction with inverse rendering for general rigidly moving objects. Our experimental analysis on synthetic data demonstrates that motion can be an advantage for disentangling material and lighting: the reconstructed material is significantly more accurate when the object is observed under rigid motion than when it is static. Moreover, results on RGB videos of real hand-held objects show that our pipeline preserves this advantage even under noisy real-world conditions.

</details>


### [46] [Simon-SR: Spatially Adaptive Modulation and Visual Prompt Adaptation for Text-Reinforced Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09351)
*Haotong Cheng,Yuxuan Li,Zijie Cui,Rongling Tan,Chenyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Simon-SR 是一个面向单图像超分辨率的多模态框架，通过可学习提示实现高效语义挖掘与鲁棒的文本-图像融合，在多个指标上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态 SISR 方法虽能提升感知质量，但容易受到错误先验影响，并且通常依赖昂贵的标注数据；因此需要一种更稳健、标注成本更低的语义引导超分辨率方法。

Method: 提出 Simon-SR，结合对比式提示学习（Contrastive Prompt Learning）与提示引导的空间自适应细化（Prompt-Guided Spatially Adaptive Refinement），以学习语义提示、增强多模态对齐，并在重建过程中进行更可靠的文本-图像融合。

Result: 实验显示，Simon-SR 超越当前先进方法，最高提升 0.50 dB PSNR、0.0133 SSIM，并在 LPIPS 上提升 0.0695。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，可学习提示能够有效降低对人工标注和不可靠先验的依赖，并提升多模态 SISR 的重建质量与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Single Image Super-Resolution (SISR) reconstructs high-quality images from low-resolution inputs. While recent multi-modal methods improve perceptual quality, they remain sensitive to erroneous priors and require expensive annotations. To address these issues, we propose Simon-SR, a multi-modal SISR framework leveraging learnable prompts for efficient semantic mining and robust text-image fusion. Our approach combines Contrastive Prompt Learning with Prompt-Guided Spatially Adaptive Refinement to enhance multi-modal alignment. Experiments demonstrate that Simon-SR surpasses state-of-the-art methods, achieving maximum improvements of 0.50 dB in PSNR, 0.0133 in SSIM, and 0.0695 in LPIPS. Code will be released.

</details>


### [47] [CtrlVTON: Controllable Virtual Try-On via Visual-Instance-Prompt Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09362)
*Seungyong Lee,Hyun Jun Jang,Sangoh Kim,Sungjoon Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出面向可控虚拟试衣的两项贡献：VIP-SAM用于根据平铺服装图在人物照片中分割同一服装实例，CtrlVTON将虚拟试衣重构为带分割掩码控制的图像编辑任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟试衣系统虽然能较真实地把服装迁移到目标人物上，但用户难以控制穿着方式，如松紧、是否塞入、开合状态以及在身体上的空间位置。

Method: 作者首先定义Visual-Instance-Prompt Segmentation任务，并提出VIP-SAM：以服装平铺图作为视觉实例提示，在人物穿着图中分割对应服装实例。其次提出CtrlVTON框架，将虚拟试衣视为图像编辑问题，并引入分割掩码作为像素级控制信号，以约束服装布局、样式、尺寸和位置。

Result: VIP-SAM和CtrlVTON分别在对应任务上达到当前最优结果。CtrlVTON相比强大的专有图像编辑系统，能更忠实地遵循用户给定布局，同时保持相当的服装保真度。

Conclusion: 该工作通过实例级服装分割和掩码驱动的可控试衣生成，显著提升了虚拟试衣中的用户可控性与布局一致性，为可编辑、可控的VTO系统提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Virtual try-on (VTO) has made significant progress in realistically transferring garments onto a target person. Yet most systems give the user little control over how a garment should be worn -- its size (loose or fitted), style (e.g., tucked in or untucked, open or closed), and spatial placement on the body. We address this gap with two complementary contributions. First, we define and solve Visual-Instance-Prompt Segmentation via VIP-SAM: given a flatlay image of a garment, segment that specific instance in a photograph of a person wearing it. This is an instance-level task, distinct from the typically studied category-level segmentation. Second, we introduce CtrlVTON, a controllable VTO framework that recasts try-on as an image editing problem and adds segmentation masks as pixel-level control over garment layout, including style, size, and spatial placement on the body. VIP-SAM and CtrlVTON each achieve state-of-the-art results on their respective tasks. In particular, CtrlVTON generates images that follow user-provided layouts far more faithfully than the strongest proprietary editing systems while matching them on garment fidelity.

</details>


### [48] [SVF-CR: Synchronized Visual-Facial Cross-Refinement for Multimodal Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09417)
*Hyein Park,Namho Kim,Junhwa Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出SVF-CR框架，用同步的全局视觉与局部人脸片段表示进行跨注意力互相细化，并结合文本、音频进行成对证据融合，以识别矛盾与犹豫状态，在BAH公开评测集上取得0.7156 macro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矛盾和犹豫是细微行为状态，往往同时体现在语言内容、面部行为、视觉场景和声学线索中。单一模态或简单拼接难以充分捕捉这些线索之间的时间同步关系与交互，因此需要一种能建模跨模态时序对齐证据交互的方法。

Method: 方法首先用相同时间划分提取整段视频的视觉片段token和裁剪人脸片段token；随后通过模态内自注意力和双向视觉-人脸交叉注意力进行同步跨细化，使全局视频上下文与局部面部行为互相增强。接着基于一致性与差异性建模构建片段级视觉-人脸证据，并经时间自注意力和注意力池化汇聚。文本与声学特征通过轻量上下文自注意力细化，最终与增强后的视觉-人脸证据进行成对证据融合以完成分类。

Result: 在BAH公开评测划分上，SVF-CR相较于全局视觉-人脸token融合和同步证据基线均有提升，达到public macro-F1 0.7156。

Conclusion: 研究表明，同步的视觉-人脸跨注意力细化与基于一致性/差异性的证据构建能更有效建模细粒度行为状态中的多模态交互，对矛盾和犹豫识别具有较好的性能增益。

Abstract: Ambivalence and hesitancy are subtle behavioral states that are expressed through a combination of verbal content, facial behavior, visual context, and acoustic cues. Effective recognition therefore requires not only extracting informative unimodal representations, but also modeling how temporally aligned behavioral evidence interacts across modalities. In this paper, we propose a synchronized visual-facial cross-refinement framework (SVF-CR) with pairwise multimodal evidence fusion for ambivalence and hesitancy recognition. The proposed method first extracts whole-video segment tokens and cropped-face segment tokens using the same temporal partition. The synchronized visual and facial tokens are refined through intra-modal self-attention and bidirectional visual-facial cross-attention, allowing whole-video context and local facial behavior to mutually refine each other before evidence construction. We then construct segment-level visual-facial evidence using consistency and discrepancy modeling, followed by temporal self-attention and attention pooling. Textual and acoustic features are lightly refined through context self-attention and are fused with the enhanced visual-facial evidence at the final decision stage using pairwise evidence fusion. Experiments on the BAH (Behavioral Ambivalence/Hesitancy) public evaluation split show that the proposed synchronized visual-facial cross-refinement improves public macro-F1 over both global visual-face token fusion and synchronized evidence baselines, achieving a public macro-F1 of 0.7156. Code is available at : https://github.com/hiinnnii/BAH-Challenge-ECCV2026\_SVF-CR.

</details>


### [49] [Multimodal Scenario Similarity Search for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09428)
*Tamás Matuszka,András Tamásy,Balázs Szolár*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估一种用于自动驾驶场景检索的多模态框架，融合视觉表征与轨迹表征，结果表明二者互补，联合使用可获得最佳检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模自动驾驶数据集中包含海量记录场景，需要高效检索与查询场景相似的案例。现有方法多依赖视觉表征或运动描述，缺乏对二者优劣及互补性的系统比较。

Method: 构建统一的多模态场景检索流程，结合视觉嵌入和基于轨迹的表征。重点研究两类轨迹方法：基于周围智能体运动显式匹配的 Exo-Trajectory，以及通过对目标轨迹进行对比学习得到表征的 Transformer 方法 ScenarioFormer，并与强视觉基线进行对比。

Result: 轨迹表征在以运动为核心的事件中表现突出，如切入、转弯、交通排队等；视觉嵌入在外观线索更具信息量的场景中表现更好。融合视觉与轨迹信息能够稳定提升检索质量，并取得总体最佳性能。

Conclusion: 外观相似性与运动相似性刻画了自动驾驶场景相似性的互补维度。多模态检索系统有助于自动驾驶数据挖掘、数据集整理以及基于场景的验证。

Abstract: Large-scale autonomous-driving datasets contain vast numbers of recorded scenarios, creating a need for efficient retrieval methods that can identify situations similar to a given query. Existing approaches typically rely on either visual representations or motion-based descriptions, making it difficult to understand their relative strengths and limitations for scenario retrieval. In this work, we present a multimodal framework for autonomous-driving scenario retrieval that combines visual and trajectory-based representations within a unified retrieval pipeline. We investigate two trajectory-based approaches: Exo-Trajectory, an explicit matching method based on surrounding-agent motion, and ScenarioFormer, a transformer-based representation learned from object trajectories using contrastive learning. We compare these approaches against strong vision-based baselines and analyze their behavior across a diverse set of driving scenarios. Experimental results show that trajectory representations provide strong retrieval performance for motion-centric events such as cut-ins, turning maneuvers, and traffic queueing, while visual embeddings excel when appearance cues are informative. Most importantly, combining visual and trajectory information consistently improves retrieval quality, yielding the best overall performance. These findings demonstrate that appearance and motion capture are complementary notions of scenario similarity and motivate multimodal retrieval systems for autonomous-driving data mining, dataset curation, and scenario-based validation.

</details>


### [50] [Parameter-Efficient Vision-Language Adaptation with Continuous Metadata Conditioning for Animal Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09443)
*Anil Osman Tur,Tonje Knutsen Sordalen,Kim Tallaksen Halvorsen,Cigdem Beyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向长期动物重识别的参数高效 CLIP 适配框架，核心是连续元数据条件化提示机制，在训练中利用数值元数据提升对外观随时间变化的鲁棒性，推理时仍仅需视觉输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期动物 ReID 面临个体形态逐渐演化、季节性外观变化以及身份/时间分布偏移等问题；现有视觉-语言模型虽具备强预训练表征，但难以直接适配纵向生态数据场景，尤其是在测试时间分布变化或开放集条件下。

Method: 方法基于 CLIP 进行参数高效适配，结合低秩视觉适配、提示监督和跨模态对齐；主要创新是连续元数据条件化机制，将数值属性直接融入 prompt 表征，而非离散化为文本类别，从而在训练阶段平滑调制嵌入空间，同时保持测试阶段为纯视觉推理。

Result: 在一个跨七年的鱼类纵向数据集及多个野生动物基准上进行实验，覆盖闭集、开放集和时间感知评估协议；结果显示该方法在长期外观变化和时间分布偏移下取得更优性能。

Conclusion: 连续元数据条件化能有效提升动物长期 ReID 的时序鲁棒性；参数高效 CLIP 适配在不增加测试时元数据依赖的情况下改善泛化能力，适合纵向生态监测应用。

Abstract: Long-term animal re-identification (ReID) must remain robust to gradual morphological evolution and seasonal appearance shifts. Although recent vision-language models provide strong pretrained visual representations, adapting them to longitudinal ecological settings remains challenging, particularly under identity and temporal distribution shifts. We present a parameter-efficient CLIP adaptation framework for animal ReID and introduce a continuous metadata-conditioning mechanism that incorporates numerical attributes directly into the prompt representation during training. While low-rank visual adaptation, prompt-based supervision, and cross-modal alignment provide the adaptation framework, the proposed metadata-conditioning strategy constitutes the primary methodological contribution. By preserving the continuous structure of numerical metadata rather than discretizing it into textual categories, the proposed approach enables smooth modulation of the embedding space during training while maintaining a purely visual inference pipeline. Experiments on a seven-year longitudinal fish dataset and multiple wildlife benchmarks demonstrate improved performance under closed-set, open-set, and time-aware evaluation protocols. The results demonstrate that continuous metadata conditioning improves robustness to longitudinal appearance variation and temporal distribution shifts, while parameter-efficient adaptation enables a purely visual inference pipeline without requiring metadata at test time. Code and evaluation splits can be found at: https://github.com/AnilOsmanTur/MetaPrompt-ReID.

</details>


### [51] [Robustifying Vision-Language Models via Test-Time Prompt Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09450)
*Xingyu Zhu,Huanshen Wu,Shuo Wang,Beier Zhu,Jiannan Ge,Jiaheng Zhang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 RITA，一种面向预训练视觉-语言模型的鲁棒测试时提示自适应框架，通过分布级对齐提升对抗扰动下的零样本鲁棒性，同时保持干净样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 等 VLM 具备较强零样本泛化能力，但在对抗扰动下性能显著下降。现有测试时自适应方法多依赖样本级置信度启发式，难以区分“高置信度的对抗错误预测”和真正的语义一致性，因此鲁棒性受限。作者观察到，对抗扰动虽然会破坏整体表征，但增强视图分布中仍可能保留语义完整性。

Method: RITA 将测试时提示自适应从样本级估计转向分布级对齐：首先对输入构造增强视图并提取视觉特征分布；然后利用最优传输将增强视觉特征分布与文本原型进行对齐，以缓解对抗离群点并修正跨模态语义错位；此外，引入动态缓存机制，从测试流中逐步积累可靠线索，用于在线细化和更新。

Result: 大量实验表明，RITA 能显著提升 VLM 在对抗扰动场景下的鲁棒性，并且不会牺牲干净数据上的准确率。

Conclusion: 该工作强调分布结构比单样本置信度更适合用于对抗场景下的测试时自适应；通过最优传输和动态缓存，RITA 为提升 VLM 鲁棒性提供了一种有效的分布级提示自适应方案。

Abstract: Pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as CLIP achieve strong zero-shot generalization, but their performance degrades sharply under adversarial perturbations. Existing test-time adaptation methods typically rely on sample-level confidence heuristics, overlooking the intrinsic distributional structure of the data. This sample-centric approach limits robustness, as it fails to distinguish confident adversarial mispredictions from true semantic consistency. In this work, we observe that adversarial distortion is structurally brittle: while holistic representations are corrupted, semantic integrity is often preserved in the distribution of augmented views. Motivated by this insight, we propose RITA, a Robust test-tIme prompt-TAdaptation framework that shifts from sample-level estimates to distribution-level alignment. Specifically, RITA employs optimal transport to align the distribution of augmented visual features with textual prototypes, mitigating adversarial outliers and rectifying cross-modal semantic misalignment. Furthermore, we introduce a dynamic cache to progressively accumulate reliable cues from the test stream for online refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RITA significantly improves adversarial robustness without compromising clean accuracy.

</details>


### [52] [Hydra++: Real-Time Hierarchical 3D Scene Graph Construction With Object-Level Shape Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09455)
*Hyungtae Lim,Nathan Hughes,Xihang Yu,Ruihan Xu,Yun Chang,Jingnan Shi,Rajat Talak,Luca Carlone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hydra++ 将学习式实例级形状估计集成到分层 3D 场景图中，提升物体与场景重建质量，并支持室外大规模混合传感器运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 场景图通常使用部分点云或类别级 CAD 模板表示物体几何，难以表达实例特异的精细形状；同时室外场景中的深度数据稀疏且噪声较大，限制了场景级重建质量。

Method: 提出 Hydra++ 系统，将类别无关的形状估计器接入分层 3D 场景图流水线；引入重投影掩码一致性检查以剔除由部分观测或分割误差导致的退化预测；默认采用 CRISP 实现在线构图，并评估 SAM3D 等较慢估计器作为模块化替代；同时支持 LiDAR-相机混合配置以适应大规模室外环境。

Result: 在仿真与真实室外校园场景中的实验表明，Hydra++ 在物体级和场景级重建质量上均优于传统粗粒度几何表示方法；不同形状估计器展示了泛化能力与延迟之间的权衡。

Conclusion: Hydra++ 证明了将学习式形状估计纳入 3D 场景图管线的可行性与价值，可在保持在线构图能力的同时提升实例级几何表达，并通过混合传感器配置增强室外大规模重建效果。

Abstract: 3D scene graphs provide a hierarchical abstraction of environments by encoding spatial entities, such as objects and places, and their relationships. However, existing scene graph systems model object geometry coarsely, relying on partial point clouds or class-level CAD templates, which limits instance-specific shape detail. This paper presents Hydra++, a system-level investigation into how learning-based object shape estimators can be integrated into a hierarchical 3D scene graph pipeline. Hydra++ incorporates category-agnostic shape estimation and a reprojection-mask consistency check to reject degenerate predictions from partial observations or imprecise segmentation. In its default CRISP-based configuration, Hydra++ performs online scene graph construction; slower estimators such as SAM3D are evaluated as modular alternatives to demonstrate generalization-latency trade-offs. Furthermore, to address the challenges of sparse and noisy depth measurements in outdoor environments, Hydra++ supports a hybrid LiDAR-camera configuration for large-scale operation, improving scene-level reconstruction quality. Experiments in both simulation and real-world outdoor campus scenarios demonstrate that Hydra++ improves object- and scene-level reconstruction quality. Project page is available at https://hydra-plusplus.github.io/.

</details>


### [53] [Foveation-Guided Dynamic Token Selection for Robust and Efficient Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09480)
*Ibrahim Batuhan Akkaya,Kishaan Jeeveswaran,Bahram Zonooz,Elahe Arani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Foveated Dynamic Transformer（FDT），一种受人类视觉系统启发的动态token选择视觉Transformer，在保持或提升精度的同时降低计算量，并展现出对噪声和对抗扰动的内在鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉系统通过中央凹采样与眼动机制，以较低代谢和计算成本实现高效、鲁棒、适应性的视觉感知。论文希望将这种机制引入视觉Transformer，以解决传统ViT计算开销较大、对无关信息敏感以及鲁棒性不足的问题。

Method: 提出FDT架构，包含注视模块和中央凹化模块。注视模块预测或选择关键注视点，用于筛除不相关信息并进行动态token选择；中央凹化模块围绕注视区域生成具有多尺度信息的foveated embeddings，从而在Transformer中实现自适应计算与信息聚合。

Result: 在50% fixation-budget设置下，FDT相较DeiT-S取得更高准确率（81.9% vs. 80.9%），同时减少34.57%的MACs。此外，即使未针对噪声或对抗攻击进行显式训练，FDT仍表现出较强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FDT证明了将人类视觉系统中的注视与中央凹机制融入视觉Transformer，可以在准确率、计算效率和鲁棒性之间取得更优权衡，是构建自适应且更可靠视觉神经网络的一种有前景方向。

Abstract: The human visual system (HVS) employs foveated sampling and eye movements to achieve efficient perception, conserving both metabolic energy and computational resources. Drawing inspiration from this robustness and adaptability, we introduce the Foveated Dynamic Transformer (FDT), a foveation-guided dynamic token-selection architecture that integrates these mechanisms into a vision transformer framework. The FDT exhibits strong resilience to various types of noise and adversarial attacks, despite not being explicitly trained for such challenges. This inherent robustness is achieved through the use of fixation and foveation modules: the fixation module identifies fixation points to filter out irrelevant information, while the foveation module generates foveated embeddings with multi-scale information. At the 50% fixation-budget setting, FDT achieves higher accuracy than DeiT-S (81.9% vs. 80.9%) while reducing multiply-accumulate operations by 34.57%, highlighting one operating point on its accuracy-efficiency trade-off. These attributes position FDT as an HVS-inspired step toward artificial neural networks that combine adaptive computation with improved resilience.

</details>


### [54] [Decoupling Language Guidance from Backbones for Text-Guided Medical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09481)
*Yungeng Liu,Xuanzi Fang,Haijin Zeng,Qi Dai,Yongyong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BTHA提出一种可在不同视觉/文本骨干间迁移的层级适配器框架，用于文本引导医学图像分割，在保持解码器接口稳定的同时提升分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导医学图像分割方法通常将跨模态融合、监督策略和解码器结构紧密绑定到特定架构，导致语言引导模块难以复用于不同视觉与文本编码器组合；一旦更换骨干网络，往往需要重新设计整体网络。

Method: 论文提出BTHA框架，以多尺度视觉特征和文本表示为稳定特征级接口，通过保持形状不变的适配器注入语义信息，并维持解码器侧张量契约。其核心包括层级粗到细监督策略：全局图文对齐、多尺度辅助定位、边界感知最终掩码细化；以及SAGSG适配器，通过尺度自适应门控控制不同分辨率下的文本注入，并用通道重校准抑制冗余跨模态响应。

Result: 在多种视觉骨干和文本骨干上评估显示，同一适配器与监督设计可适配卷积式和Transformer式视觉编码器以及不同语言编码器；在四个公开数据集上的实验表明，BTHA在较小计算开销下优于强文本引导基线。

Conclusion: BTHA通过稳定接口、层级监督和尺度自适应语义注入，提高了文本引导医学图像分割方法的骨干可迁移性、复用性和分割效果，是一种模块化且计算开销适中的跨模态分割框架。

Abstract: Text-guided medical image segmentation leverages clinical semantics to improve lesion delineation, yet many existing models bind cross-modal fusion, supervision, and decoder design into a task-specific architecture. Such tight coupling makes it difficult to reuse language guidance modules across heterogeneous vision and text backbones, and often requires redesigning the network when the encoder pair changes. This paper presents BTHA, a backbone-transferable hierarchical adapter framework for text-guided medical image segmentation. BTHA is built around a stable feature-level interface: given multi-scale visual features and a text representation, it injects semantic guidance through shape-preserving adapters while maintaining the decoder-side tensor contract. To make this interface effective, we introduce a Hierarchical Coarse-to-Fine Supervision Strategy that decomposes learning into global image-text alignment, multi-scale auxiliary localization, and boundary-aware final mask refinement. We further design a Scale-Adaptive Gated Semantic Guidance (SAGSG) adapter, where resolution-specific gates adaptively control textual injection and channel recalibration suppresses redundant cross-modal responses. Evaluations across diverse vision and text backbones show that the same adapter and supervision design remains effective across convolutional and transformer-based visual encoders as well as different language encoders. Experiments on four public datasets further demonstrate that BTHA improves strong text-guided baselines with modest computational overhead.

</details>


### [55] [What VGGT Knows About Overlap: Probing Geometric Foundation Models for Co-Visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09503)
*Filippo Ziliotto,Luciano Serafini,Lamberto Ballan,Tommaso Campari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现VGGT内部表征自发编码图像对共可视性，并提出冻结VGGT、仅训练轻量层级MoE头的Co-VGGT，用RGB输入高精度预测共可视关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D重建与机器人定位中需要判断图像对是否观测到重叠可见表面；在低重叠场景下该问题尤其困难，且对SfM/SLAM中的可视图构建至关重要。

Method: 作者分析VGGT各层表征，发现早期层构建3D感知场景表示、晚期层进行共可视推理，并识别L17为稳定的非共可视负锚点。在此基础上冻结VGGT，训练少于7.5M参数的层级混合专家分类头，将不同层视为专门专家并对输入图像对自适应加权。

Result: 在Co-VisiON基准上，Co-VGGT超过人工标注基线，相比既有方法 pairwise 提升超过25%，multiview 提升超过10%；pairwise预测校准良好，ECE=0.030。

Conclusion: VGGT这类几何基础模型中存在可解释的层级专门化与自发共可视能力；轻量头即可将其转化为实用、校准良好的共可视分类器，可直接服务于SfM和SLAM下游流程。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in 3D reconstruction and robotic localization is co-visibility: determining which image pairs share overlapping visible surfaces, particularly in scenarios with minimal overlap. We demonstrate that VGGT implicitly encodes co-visibility as an emergent behavior: without any supervision for this task, its internal representations exhibit a clear hierarchical structure mirroring that of large language models, i.e. early layers build a 3D-aware scene representation, while late layers act as dedicated co-visibility reasoners. In particular, we identify layer L17 as a negative anchor that consistently routes non-co-visible pairs for this backbone, regardless of the evaluation setting, providing task-grounded evidence of layer specialization in a geometry-grounded foundation model. Building on this, we introduce Co-VGGT, which freezes VGGT and trains only a lightweight layer-wise mixture-of-experts head (less than 7.5M parameters) to classify co-visibility from RGB alone, treating each layer as a specialized expert whose geometric abstraction is adaptively weighted per input pair. On the Co-VisiON benchmark, Co-VGGT surpasses the human annotation baseline and improves over prior work by more than 25% pairwise and 10% multiview. Pairwise predictions are well-calibrated (ECE=0.030), enabling direct use as edge weights in visibility graphs for downstream SfM and SLAM pipelines without post-hoc correction. Code and data are available.

</details>


### [56] [SigLIP-HD by Fine-to-Coarse Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09488)
*Lihe Yang,Zhen Zhao,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SigLIP-HD 通过细到粗监督，让中分辨率图像的粗粒度特征学习高分辨率图像的细粒度特征，在不增加推理预算的情况下提升视觉表征质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型通常可通过输入更高分辨率图像获得更细粒度视觉 token，但这会带来额外计算开销、多次前向传播和更复杂的后处理。论文关注的问题是：在不增大输入图像、不增加推理成本的前提下，是否还能进一步释放标准/中等分辨率下视觉编码器的感知能力。

Method: 作者提出 SigLIP-HD，基于 SigLIP 2 构建一个简单的 fine-to-coarse 监督框架：将高分辨率图像产生的细粒度特征作为监督信号，约束中分辨率图像的粗粒度特征去模仿对应高分辨率版本的细粒度表征，从而提升低成本输入下的视觉 token 质量。

Result: 在相同推理预算下，SigLIP-HD 生成了质量更高的视觉 token；在大量 MLLM 基准测试中相较基线模型表现稳定更强，尤其在 OCR 相关任务上提升明显。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，不必简单依赖更高分辨率输入，也可以通过训练阶段的特征蒸馏/监督设计增强视觉编码器的细粒度感知能力，实现更高效的多模态视觉表征。

Abstract: High-quality visual representation is a long-standing pursuit in computer vision. In the context of multimodal LLMs (MLLMs), feeding higher-resolution images can produce more fine-grained visual tokens. However, it introduces additional computational and design complexity, due to multiple forward passes and post-processing of increased tokens. Before simply adopting a higher resolution, have we truly unlocked the model's full perception capability at a standard resolution? Therefore, we study an interesting problem: how to achieve fine visual perception under lower cost without larger images. We present SigLIP-HD in this work. The core is a highly simple fine-to-coarse supervision design. We enforce the coarse feature of a mid-resolution image to mimic the fine-grained feature of its high-resolution version. We build this framework on the advanced SigLIP 2 model. Our final model produces better visual tokens at exactly the same inference budget. It is validated on extensive MLLM benchmarks and consistently delivers stronger results than our baseline model, especially on OCR-related tasks.

</details>


### [57] [Seeing is Free, Speaking is Not: Uncovering the True Energy Bottleneck in Edge VLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09520)
*Junfei Zhan,Haoxun Shen,Mingang Guo,Zixuan Huang,Tengjiao He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该摘要指出，边缘端VLM推理的主要能耗瓶颈并非视觉输入处理，而是输出解码长度；控制输出token数比视觉token剪枝更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM是具身智能的重要感知基础，但其在边缘硬件上的能耗机制尚不清楚。已有研究多集中于减少视觉token，默认视觉处理是主要能耗来源，本文旨在系统检验这一假设。

Method: 作者对五种VLM、三类架构、四种输入分辨率以及两类硬件平台（RTX 3070与Jetson Orin NX）进行系统化端侧推理能耗分析，比较输入分辨率、图像复杂度、提示类型、输入/输出token数量对功耗、延迟和总能耗的影响。

Result: 实验发现：平均推理功率基本是模型内禀常数，在分辨率、图像复杂度和prompt类型变化下波动小于5%；输出token的耗时是输入token的11到39倍；相同分辨率下图像复杂度可导致最高4.1倍能耗差异，但根源是输出长度变化；视觉token即使完全移除也最多节省约10%能耗，而控制输出长度最高可节省97%。

Conclusion: 边缘端VLM推理的核心能耗瓶颈是解码阶段和输出token数量，而非视觉token处理。未来VLM能效优化应从单纯视觉token剪枝转向输出长度控制、解码优化和面向生成阶段的能耗管理。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are the perceptual backbone of embodied AI, but their energy footprint on edge hardware remains poorly understood. Existing efficiency efforts focus predominantly on reducing visual tokens, implicitly treating visual processing as the dominant energy cost. We overturn this implicit assumption through the first systematic energy profiling of on-device VLM inference, spanning five models across three architecture families, four input resolutions, and two hardware platforms (NVIDIA RTX 3070 and Jetson Orin NX). Our analysis yields three findings. First, average inference power is a model-intrinsic constant, invariant to input resolution, image complexity, and prompt type, with less than 5% variation across all conditions. This means that all energy variation across inputs must arise from variation in inference time, not from variation in power draw. Second, each output token costs 11 to 39x more wall-clock time than each input token due to the compute-bound and memory-bound asymmetry between prefill and decode, making output token count the dominant driver of both latency and energy. Third, image complexity, measured by the number of objects in an image, induces up to 4.1x energy differences at identical resolution. This variation arises not from increased visual processing cost, but from differences in output length. These findings expose a fundamental limitation of visual token pruning: even removing all visual tokens saves at most 10% of total energy for fixed-token models. Across models spanning 1 billion to 8 billion parameters, controlling output length saves up to 97% of total energy, with the energy dominance of decoding growing stronger at larger model scale. In short, the true energy bottleneck in edge VLM inference is not what the model sees, but how much it says.

</details>


### [58] [ALICE: Learning a General-Purpose Pathology Foundation Model from Vision, Vision-Language, and Slide-Level Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09526)
*Jiawen Li,Tian Guan,Huijuan Shi,Xitong Ling,Mingxi Fu,Anjia Han,Chao He,Yonghong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALICE 是一个面向计算病理学的统一基础模型，通过多阶段聚合蒸馏将多个专用教师模型的能力整合到单一骨干网络中，并在多类下游任务中取得总体最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算病理基础模型的能力受预训练目标、数据来源和空间尺度限制，不同模型分别擅长视觉、视觉-语言或全玻片层面任务，导致互补能力分散在多个独立骨干网络中，难以形成统一、通用的病理分析模型。

Method: 论文提出 ALICE，采用多阶段聚合蒸馏方法，依次将 8 个教师模型（包括纯视觉、视觉-语言和玻片级模型）的知识蒸馏到单一骨干网络的专用模块中。模型使用 24,985,184 张 tile 级病理图像和 155,604 张高分辨率图像进行预训练，并在多层级、多任务场景中评估。

Result: ALICE 在 21 个任务场景、96 个下游任务和 48 个数据来源上进行评估，覆盖感兴趣区域组织分析、视觉-语言多模态评估以及全玻片临床评估。在三类评估设置中，ALICE 相比任务匹配的病理基础模型均获得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: 结果表明，多阶段聚合蒸馏能够有效整合不同专用模型的互补能力，使单一统一骨干网络具备广泛的计算病理应用能力。

Abstract: Foundation models are reshaping computational pathology, yet their capabilities remain shaped by pretraining objectives, data sources, and spatial scales, fragmenting complementary expertise across separate backbones. Here we present ALICE, a unified foundation model trained through multi-stage agglomerative distillation that sequentially distills eight vision-only, vision-language, and slide-level teacher models into dedicated modules of a single backbone. ALICE is pretrained on 24,985,184 tile-level pathology images and 155,604 high-resolution images, and evaluated across 21 task scenarios, 96 downstream tasks, and 48 data sources, spanning region-of-interest tissue analysis, vision-language multimodal evaluation, and whole-slide clinical assessment. In all three evaluation settings, ALICE achieved the best average rank among task-matched pathology foundation models. These results demonstrate that agglomerative distillation can consolidate complementary capabilities from specialized models into a unified backbone for broad computational pathology applications. The model is available at https://github.com/WonderLandxD/ALICE.

</details>


### [59] [DGSfM: Depth-Guided Scale-Aware Global Structure-from-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09507)
*Sithu Aung,Viktor Kocur,Yaqing Ding,Torsten Sattler,Zuzana Kukelova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DGSfM 利用单目深度先验增强全局 SfM，将尺度不确定的极几何约束转化为尺度感知的相对位姿约束，并通过图过滤、匹配剪枝、尺度平均与深度引导初始化提升重建鲁棒性和位姿精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全局 SfM 虽然高效，但依赖尺度不确定的极几何，导致全局定位容易受到基线估计噪声、视图图约束弱以及视觉歧义产生的错误边影响，从而降低相机位姿和稀疏三维结构恢复质量。

Method: 论文提出 DGSfM：首先为每对图像引入单目深度图，使用深度感知相对位姿求解器获得尺度感知的相对位姿约束；其次进行视图图过滤和基于深度一致性的对应点剪枝，以去除伪边和仅满足极几何但不满足深度一致性的误匹配；最后采用全局尺度平均和深度引导的位姿-点初始化，将单目深度统一到公共重建尺度，并为全局定位与束调整提供稳定初始化。

Result: 在 ETH3D 和 IMC2021 数据集上的实验表明，DGSfM 在稀疏与稠密匹配前端下均稳定优于强全局 SfM 基线，并在相机位姿精度方面取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，单目深度可作为可扩展先验有效缓解全局 SfM 中的尺度歧义和错误匹配问题，同时保留显式多视图优化框架，从而提升全局重建的鲁棒性与准确性。

Abstract: Global Structure-from-Motion (SfM) is an efficient paradigm for recovering camera poses and sparse 3D structure from unordered images. However, its reliance on scale-ambiguous epipolar geometry makes global positioning sensitive to noisy baseline estimates and weak view-graph constraints, while false edges from visually ambiguous pairs can further degrade reconstruction. We propose DGSfM, a depth-aware global SfM pipeline that uses monocular depth maps as a scalable prior while preserving explicit multi-view optimization. For each image pair, we use a depth-aware relative pose solver to convert scale-ambiguous epipolar constraints into scale-aware relative pose constraints. We further improve robustness through view-graph filtering and depth-consistency-based correspondence pruning, which suppress false edges and matches that remain plausible under epipolar geometry alone. Finally, global scale averaging and depth-guided pose-point initialization align monocular depth maps into a common reconstruction scale and provide stable initialization for global positioning and bundle adjustment. Experiments on ETH3D and IMC2021 show that DGSfM consistently improves over strong global SfM baselines across sparse and dense matching front-ends, achieving substantial gains in pose accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/sithu31296/DGSfM.

</details>


### [60] [TCLA: Training-Free Class-wise Logit Adaptation for Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09562)
*Tianyou Jiang,Ziyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 TCLA，一种无需训练的少样本适配方法，用少量支持样本校正医学视觉语言模型的推理 logits，以提升域外泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学 VLM 虽具备较强零样本能力，但在域外数据上会因域偏移和预训练带来的类别偏置而性能下降；现有少样本适配方法通常引入可训练模块，在极低样本条件下不稳定且跨医学数据鲁棒性不足。

Method: TCLA 不训练额外参数，而是在推理阶段利用少量 support samples 对模型输出 logits 进行校正，目标是增强类间区分、缓解类别混淆，并降低目标域与预训练域之间的分布差异；该方法快速且与具体模型无关。

Result: 在涵盖 X-ray、Ultrasound、MRI、CT、Histopathology 等多种医学影像模态的九个数据集上，TCLA 持续提升医学 VLM 的 OOD 表现，并在多数情况下优于已有训练式适配方法。

Conclusion: TCLA 表明，针对医学 VLM 的少样本域外适配并不一定依赖参数训练；通过支持集驱动的推理校正即可获得稳定、快速且泛化性较好的性能提升。

Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit strong zero-shot performance, yet their effectiveness still declines on out-of-distribution (OOD) data due to domain shifts and class bias inherited from large-scale pretraining. Existing few-shot adaptation methods typically introduce additional trainable components, which can be unstable in extremely low-data regimes (e.g., 1-shot), and lack robustness on different medical data. We present TCLA, a purely training-free few-shot adaptation method for Medical VLMs, which is fast and model-agnostic. TCLA corrects inference logits based on a small set of support samples, boosting pretrained VLMs performance by improving inter-class deconfusion and reducing domain shift. Extensive experiments on nine datasets across multiple medical imaging modalities including X-ray, Ultrasound, MRI, CT, Histopathology, demonstrate that TCLA consistently improves OOD performance of Medical VLMs and, in most of cases, outperforms existing training-based adaptation methods.

</details>


### [61] [The Count Is There, but Misaligned: Understanding and Correcting Counting Failures in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09544)
*Ahmed Oumar El-Shangiti,Abzal Nurgazy,Hilal AlQuabeh,Nikolai Rozanov,Kentaro Inui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究VLM在物体计数中“知道但说错”的现象，并通过激活探针、因果干预和选择性自纠正提升计数性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉-语言模型在多模态任务中表现强，但在基础物体计数上仍常出错。作者希望判断这类错误源于模型内部缺乏正确计数知识，还是内部表征与最终语言输出之间存在读出/对齐缺口。

Method: 作者在四个VLM和五个计数数据集上训练简单探针分析模型激活；比较基于真实计数与基于模型输出训练的探针，并用SVCCA分析二者激活子空间关系；进一步采用因果 steering 干预增强计数相关方向；最后提出 detector-guided self-correction，在内部错误检测器预测失败时才重新提示模型。

Result: 非线性探针能够可靠检测计数错误，说明模型即使输出错误也常编码了正确计数信息。SVCCA显示真实计数探针与输出探针共享部分子空间，但读出方向不一致。增强计数识别方向可因果性提升计数表现。选择性重提示方法在无需参数更新的情况下，将计数准确率最多提升15.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 论文表明VLM计数错误并不总是由于缺乏内部知识，而可能来自内部计数表征到语言输出的错位；基于激活的错误探测既可作为改进计数的实用推理时工具，也可作为理解内部知识与输出差距的机制性分析手段。

Abstract: Despite strong performance on many multimodal tasks, vision-language models (VLMs) still struggle with basic object counting. We investigate whether this reflects missing internal knowledge or a gap between internal representations and verbalized outputs. Training simple probes on activations from four VLMs across five counting datasets reveals that nonlinear probes can reliably detect counting errors, suggesting that VLMs often encode the correct count even when they output the wrong answer. SVCCA analysis shows that probes trained on ground-truth counts and probes trained on model outputs occupy a partially shared activation subspace but read out along misaligned directions. We further validate our findings using a causal steering intervention, proving that strengthening the direction of count-identified probes does improve model counting performance. Motivated by this result, we propose a detector-guided self-correction method that selectively re-prompts the model only when an internal error detector predicts failure. This simple inference-time intervention improves counting accuracy by up to 15.6 absolute percentage points, without any parameter updates. Our results establish activation-based error probing as both a practical tool for improving VLM counting and a mechanistic lens on the gap between internal knowledge and model outputs.

</details>


### [62] [4DR360: State Reasoning for Joint 3D Detection and Occupancy Prediction in 4D Radar-Camera Full-Scene Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09629)
*Xiaokai Bai,Lianqing Zheng,Runwei Guan,Songkai Wang,Siyuan Cao,Hui-liang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向360°全场景感知的4D毫米波雷达-相机融合框架，将语义占用建模为可传播的场景状态，并在统一多任务协议下评估检测与占用感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠自动驾驶需要同时理解前景目标与稠密语义布局。4D毫米波雷达具备鲁棒、低成本优势，但点云回波稀疏，必须与相机融合；现有方法多聚焦目标检测，检测与占用双任务之间交互不足。

Method: 提出跨模态状态推理范式，将语义占用作为持续场景状态而非最终输出；通过阶段式传播实现由粗到细的特征聚合。核心模块包括State-guided BEV Enhancement用于增强单帧BEV表示，以及Doppler-guided Temporal Fusion用于利用多普勒信息进行长时域状态证据保留。同时扩展ManTruckScenes的卫星地图生成占用标签，并与OmniHD-Scenes形成统一跨数据集检测-占用评测协议。

Result: 实验覆盖准确性、鲁棒性、消融和效率，在雷达-相机多任务评估框架下验证所提方法的有效性；摘要未给出具体数值结果。

Conclusion: 该工作推动了4D雷达-相机融合从单一检测任务走向检测与语义占用联合建模，强调占用状态的时序持续性和跨模态推理，对自动驾驶全场景感知具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Reliable autonomous driving requires full-scene perception that couples foreground objects with dense semantic layout. Recently, 4D millimeter-wave radar has emerged as a robust and affordable sensor, yet its sparse returns make radar-camera fusion necessary for comprehensive scene understanding. Existing radar-camera methods mainly optimize detection, while dual-task systems usually decode boxes and occupancy with limited interaction. To address this gap and advance radar-based multi-task learning, we propose \method, a 4D radar-camera framework for 360$^\circ$ full-scene perception, which models semantic occupancy as a persistent scene state rather than a terminal output. \method{} follows a cross-modal state reasoning paradigm, where the occupancy state is modeled and propagated through stages for coarse-to-fine feature aggregation. Specifically, State-guided BEV Enhancement (SBE) strengthens intra-frame BEV representation, while Doppler-guided Temporal Fusion (DTF) preserves state evidence over longer temporal horizons. Beyond the model, we further extend ManTruckScenes with satellite-map-based generated occupancy labels and pair it with OmniHD-Scenes in a unified cross-dataset detection-and-occupancy protocol. The resulting experiments cover accuracy, robustness, ablation, and efficiency under one radar-camera multi-task evaluation framework. Code and labels will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [63] [Evolution of Accuracy and Visual-Cognitive Errors in a Decade of Vision-Language AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09654)
*Shravan Murlidaran,Miguel P. Eckstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出CSB复杂社会行为数据集，用于评估十年来视觉语言模型在复杂场景描述中的进展，并与MS-COCO及人类描述进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评测多依赖简单场景，缺少对复杂人类互动/行为的系统评估，也较少分析模型错误类型，因此难以全面衡量模型视觉推理与场景理解能力。

Method: 构建包含100张复杂社会互动/行为图像的CSB数据集；评估2017—2025年间4个pre-MLLM和5个MLLM的场景描述表现；将模型与20个人类描述相对于黄金标准进行比较，并在CSB与MS-COCO样本上分析五类视觉-认知错误：目标检测、识别、幻觉、场景理解和空间依赖。

Result: CSB上的描述准确率提升比MS-COCO更明显；pre-MLLM显著低于排名靠后的人类描述，而MLLM达到与排名靠前的人类描述相近的水平。MLLM基本消除了简单场景与复杂行为场景之间的准确率差距，并几乎消除了多数错误类型，但仍偶尔出现空间依赖错误。检测、识别和幻觉错误对准确率影响最大。

Conclusion: 研究表明，过去十年VLM尤其是MLLM在复杂社会行为场景描述方面取得显著进步；CSB为评估复杂视觉社会理解提供了更具挑战性的基准，同时指出空间依赖仍是未来改进方向。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) have made remarkable progress in visual reasoning during the last decade. Most evaluations have used simple scenes (MS-COCO) that do not showcase complex human interactions or behaviors, only a handful of non-curated human descriptions as a benchmark, and have not focused on understanding the model's error types. Here, we introduce the Complex Social Behavior (CSB) dataset, containing 100 images depicting complex social interactions/behaviors. We analyze the progression of scene descriptions over a decade (2017-2025) of VLMs (four pre-Multimodal Large Language Models, MLLMs, and five MLLMs). We evaluate the accuracy of the models and 20 human descriptions relative to a gold standard on the CSB dataset and on a sample from MS-COCO. We analyzed five visual-cognitive error types: object detection, recognition, hallucination, scene understanding, and spatial dependence. The CSB dataset showed a more pronounced improvement than MS-COCO in scene description accuracy, with pre-MLLMs achieving much lower accuracy than the bottom-ranked human descriptions and MLLMs attaining accuracies similar to the top-ranked human descriptions. We show that MLLMs have eliminated the gap in scene description accuracy between simpler MS-COCO scenes and scenes depicting complex behaviors (CSB). MLLMs have almost eliminated all error types in our tested datasets, except for occasionally relying on different image regions for scene descriptions than humans do (spatial dependence error). We also show that detection, recognition, and hallucination errors have the highest impact on scene description accuracy. Together, our findings provide a more thorough evaluation of how visual language models have advanced over the last decade.

</details>


### [64] [Wan-Dancer: A Hierarchical Framework for Minute-scale Coherent Music-to-Dance Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09581)
*Mingyang Huang,Peng Zhang,Li Hu,Guangyuan Wang,Bang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种层次化音乐到舞蹈视频生成框架，可生成超过一分钟的720p/30fps高稳定性舞蹈视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在长时视频生成中通常受限于约20秒时长，音乐驱动舞蹈生成容易出现时间漂移、人物身份不一致和动作重复等问题，难以实现长时、高清且节奏同步的视频生成。

Method: 方法将生成过程解耦为全局关键帧规划与局部时序细化，利用完整音乐轨道上下文保持长程一致性；引入基于时间映射RoPE嵌入的动态帧率自适应实现精确节奏对齐，采用光流损失增强运动连续性，并通过运动速度控制在快速动作中保留高保真细节。

Result: 实验表明，该框架突破传统时长限制，可生成超过一分钟的稳定720p/30fps视频，在时间稳定性方面优于现有方法，并能适配五种舞蹈类型，支持音频与文本提示条件控制。

Conclusion: 该工作在长时一致性、节奏同步和高清舞蹈视频合成方面取得显著改进，为长视频音乐驱动生成建立了新的先进水平。

Abstract: Generating long-duration, high-definition, and rhythmically synchronized dance videos directly from music remains a significant challenge, primarily due to the temporal constraints of current diffusion models, which typically fail beyond 20 seconds. Existing approaches, whether they rely on intermediate 3D skeletons or on end-to-end video synthesis, suffer from temporal drift, identity inconsistency, and repetitive motion patterns when extended to longer horizons. To address these limitations, we propose a novel hierarchical framework for minute-scale coherent music-to-dance generation. Our method decouples the process into global keyframe planning and local temporal refinement, leveraging full-track musical context to ensure long-range coherence. Key innovations include dynamic frame rate adaptation via time-mapped RoPE embeddings for precise alignment, an optical-flow-based loss function to enhance motion continuity, and motion-speed control to preserve high-fidelity details during rapid movements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework surpasses the conventional duration barrier, generating stable, 720p/30fps videos exceeding one minute with superior temporal stability. Furthermore, the model exhibits robust versatility across five distinct dance genres, conditioned on both audio and textual prompts, establishing a new state-of-the-art in coherent, long-form dance video synthesis.

</details>


### [65] [Scalable Visual Pretraining for Language Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09657)
*Yiming Zhang,Zhonghan Zhao,Wenwei Zhang,Haiteng Zhao,Tianyang Lin,Yunhua Zhou,Demin Song,Kuikun Liu,Haochen Ye,Haian Huang,Yuzhe Gu,Haijun Lv,Qipeng Guo,Bin Liu,Gaoang Wang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文主张并验证：直接利用文档视觉形态进行无监督视觉预训练，能够比将同一语料转成纯文本再预训练更有效地提升基础模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型预训练主要依赖大规模纯文本语料，但许多知识存在于图表、排版公式、页面布局等视觉表达中。将文档和网页转换为纯文本会丢失这些关键信息，因此需要重新审视“语言模型必须基于文本表示训练”的默认假设。

Method: 作者系统研究了无需文本抽取的无监督视觉预训练范式，直接使用视觉文档作为训练数据，并在多个模型骨干和评测基准上，与基于相同底层语料的文本-only预训练方法进行对比。

Result: 实验显示，在相同语料来源下，视觉预训练在多个骨干网络和基准任务上持续优于纯文本预训练，表明视觉文档中的布局、公式和图形信息能为模型学习提供额外且有效的知识信号。

Conclusion: 视觉预训练是一种可扩展的基础模型智能学习路径，能够更完整地利用文档中的多模态知识，并为提升语言智能提供比传统文本-only预训练更高效的方案。

Abstract: The rapid progress of large foundation models has been driven predominantly by pretraining on large-scale text corpora. However, many forms of knowledge are conveyed through visual representations, where figures, typeset equations, and page layouts carry rich information that cannot be faithfully or completely captured by text alone. Yet current pretraining approaches discard these visual cues by converting visually rich sources, such as documents and web pages, into plain text for learning language intelligence. This paper challenges the default assumption that language models must be trained on text-only representations and shows that Visual Pretraining is a scalable learner for foundation model intelligence. To this end, we conduct a systematic study of unsupervised visual pretraining paradigms that directly leverage visual documents without text extraction. Across multiple backbones and benchmarks, visual pretraining on the same underlying corpora consistently outperforms text-only pretraining, offering an efficient pathway to scalable language intelligence.

</details>


### [66] [Promptable Concept Segmentation from Above: Evaluating SAM 3's Zero-Shot and One-Shot Capabilities in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09583)
*Mohammad Dabaja,Turgay Celik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该摘要评估 SAM 3 在遥感影像中的零样本/单样本泛化能力，发现视觉提示有助于几何对齐，而文本提示会引入语义偏差并损害定位；模型在分割泛化上较稳健，但受分辨率与俯视语义理解限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型基础视觉模型被期待实现开放词汇、免训练的计算机视觉，但其在地球观测/遥感影像这类具有复杂俯视几何结构的分布外场景中的能力尚未被充分量化。尤其是 SAM 3 在专业领域表现不均，促使作者系统评估其遥感适用性。

Method: 作者在严格零样本与单样本约束下，围绕遥感场景分类、目标检测和实例分割开展多任务实证评估；将 SAM 3 解耦的二值存在性头改造成独立零样本分类器；通过五种配置隔离文本提示与视觉提示，分析多模态解码器的对齐机制；并提出免训练的广义零样本代理评估协议用于场景分类和实例分割基准测试。

Result: 实验发现明显的跨模态干扰：视觉提示能帮助解码器适配复杂遥感几何结构，而文本提示会注入与俯视图像不匹配的地面视角语义偏置，降低坐标回归质量。SAM 3 在分割任务中取得较高 Harmonic Mean，显示出较好的泛化与较少过拟合，但在检测/定位等方面仍受亚像素分辨率与俯视语义盲区限制。

Conclusion: SAM 3 具备一定训练免费遥感泛化潜力，特别是在实例分割任务中表现稳健，但其多模态解码器仍不能充分适配地理空间影像的几何和语义特征。未来需要面向遥感的参数高效微调，以改善文本-视觉对齐、定位精度和俯视语义理解。

Abstract: The deployment of large-scale foundation models, such as the Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3), promises a transition toward open-vocabulary, training-free computer vision. However, their capacity to generalize out-of-distribution to the complex, top-down geometric structures of Earth Observation imagery remains largely unquantified. Driven by SAM 3's performance disparities in highly specialized domains, we present a comprehensive, multi-task empirical evaluation across remote sensing scene classification, object detection, and instance segmentation under strict zero-shot and one-shot constraints. To achieve this, we introduce a structural adaptation of SAM 3 by repurposing its decoupled binary presence head into a standalone zero-shot classifier. Furthermore, by systematically isolating textual and visual prompt modalities across five configurations, we explicitly diagnose the alignment mechanics within the model's multimodal decoder. Our findings reveal severe cross-modal interference: while visual prompts successfully align the decoder to complex remote sensing geometry, textual prompts inject misaligned, ground-level semantic bias, actively degrading coordinate regression. To benchmark these capabilities without resource-intensive training, we formulate a novel training-free proxy evaluation protocol for Generalized Zero-Shot tasks (scene classification and instance segmentation). Ultimately, our results demonstrate that SAM 3 avoids the overfitting commonly seen in legacy domain-adapted models, achieving high Harmonic Mean scores in segmentation tasks. However, it remains fundamentally constrained by sub-pixel resolution limits and overhead semantic blind spots, charting a definitive mandate for parameter-efficient geospatial fine-tuning of its multimodal decoder.

</details>


### [67] [The Effects of Synthetic Data and Label Distribution on Canola Branch Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09630)
*Amirsalar Darvishpour,Mikolaj Cieslak,Adam Runions*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究在油菜分枝计数任务中，合成植物图像如何与真实图像配比及如何设置标签分布，以提升自动表型分析模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工标注植物图像成本高、耗时长；植物生长模拟器可生成带精确标签的合成图像，但合成数据是否有效取决于合成/真实数据比例及合成标签分布，亟需系统量化。

Method: 使用校准后的 L-system 植物模型生成合成图像，在油菜分枝计数任务上训练 ResNet-18，并独立改变合成到真实图像比例以及合成数据的标签分布，比较平均绝对差等性能指标。

Result: 合成/真实比例在 1:5 至 1:22 范围内普遍提升性能，最佳比例 1:7 相比仅用真实数据将平均绝对差降低 7.6%。标签分布方面，均匀分布效果较差；向真实分布插值 90% 后平均绝对差为 0.927；对真实标签分布进行高斯平滑获得最佳结果，平均绝对差为 0.912，相比真实数据训练提升 14.7%。每个标签至少 10 张合成图像可带来适度收益，而 100 张会过度校正并损害性能。

Conclusion: 合成数据能有效改善植物表型计数模型，但收益高度依赖合成/真实比例和标签分布设计；与真实标签分布相近且适度平滑的合成分布优于简单均匀采样，过量合成样本可能反而降低性能。

Abstract: Collecting annotated plant images for automated phenotyping is often slow and expensive. Plant models simulating growth and development can generate unlimited synthetic images with exact labels. However, previous work has established that whether incorporating synthetic data improves performance depends on the ratio of synthetic to real images and the label distribution of the synthetic dataset. To systematically quantify both factors, we train ResNet-18 models on a canola branch-counting task using a calibrated L-system plant model. We vary each factor independently. Synthetic-to-real ratios of 1:5 to 1:22 broadly improve performance; the best ratio (1:7) reduces mean absolute difference by 7.6% over real-only training. For label distribution, a uniform synthetic distribution is strongly suboptimal (abs. diff. of approximately 1.70); interpolating 90% toward the real distribution yields abs. diff. 0.927, whereas Gaussian smoothing of the real label distribution yields the best overall result (abs. diff. 0.912, a 14.7% improvement over real-only). A minimum of 10 synthetic images per label offers a simpler alternative with modest gains, while 100 per label over-corrects and hurts performance.

</details>


### [68] [Revisiting Euler-Angle Regression with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09650)
*Yangting Sun,Zijun Cui,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将范围感知的欧拉角建模与 Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks（KAN）结合，用于受限关节空间中的旋转回归，并在多类任务中提升精度、收敛和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实系统如关节机器人和生物力学模型中的旋转通常由具有有界范围的欧拉角自然参数化，但欧拉角存在不连续性和奇异性，直接回归容易导致训练不稳定。作者希望重新评估欧拉角回归在特定领域约束下的有效性。

Method: 提出一种范围感知的欧拉角回归框架，并采用 KAN 替代传统固定节点激活网络。KAN 在边上学习一元函数，具有加性函数结构。论文还从理论上分析有界欧拉角范围会诱导近似加性回归结构，从而与 KAN 的归纳偏置相匹配。

Result: 在受控旋转回归、物体姿态估计、机器人逆运动学和人体逆运动学等实验中，该方法在准确率、收敛速度和计算效率方面表现出一致改进。

Conclusion: 在具有明确角度范围约束的旋转回归问题中，欧拉角并非必然劣势；若结合范围建模与适合近加性结构的 KAN，可获得稳定且高效的旋转学习效果。

Abstract: In many real-world systems, including articulated robots and biomechanical models, rotations are defined in joint space and naturally parameterized by Euler angles with bounded ranges. Yet regressing Euler angles remains challenging, as their discontinuities and singularities often destabilize training. In this work, we revisit Euler-angle regression and show that its effectiveness depends critically on the interaction between rotation representation, regression architecture, and domain constraints. We introduce a new framework that combines range-aware Euler modeling with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN), which replace fixed node-wise activations with learnable univariate functions on edges. We further provide theoretical analysis indicating that bounded Euler ranges motivate a near-additive structure in the regression function, which favors the additive functional form of KAN, and we confirm this trend empirically. Extensive experiments on controlled rotation regression, object pose estimation, and robotic and human inverse kinematics demonstrate consistent improvements in accuracy, convergence, and efficiency. The code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [69] [OpenLongTail: Generative Scaling of Long-Tail Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09655)
*Lulin Liu,Nuo Chen,Yan Wang,Bangya Liu,Wenyan Cong,Hezhen Hu,Boris Ivanovic,Hao Wang,Ziyao Zeng,Xinyu Gong,Yang Zhou,Zixiang Xiong,Dilin Wang,Zhangyang Wang,Weisong Shi,Ruohan Zhang,Marco Pavone,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenLongTail 是一个用于自动驾驶长尾事件训练的开源生成式数据引擎，可将异构野外视频补全为视角对齐、时间一致的多视角数据，从而提升闭环驾驶鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒自动驾驶策略的规模化训练受限于高质量边缘/长尾场景数据稀缺。现实世界中存在大量长尾驾驶视频，但它们来源异构，常缺少完整多视角覆盖、精确位姿或仅来自单目行车记录仪，导致难以直接用于策略学习。

Method: 论文提出 OpenLongTail，通过姿态感知的外推式视角合成流程生成缺失视角，将异构长尾视频转换为视角对齐、时间连贯的多视角资产；同时向生成引擎注入 Plücker ray 几何信息，以增强跨视角一致性和时间对齐。

Result: 通过合成异构长尾数据，模型在闭环驾驶中处理长尾事件的鲁棒性显著提升；在外推视角合成质量、位姿相关指标、视觉保真度、跨视角一致性和自车轨迹恢复方面均验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: OpenLongTail 缓解了异构长尾驾驶视频与多视角策略训练需求之间的模态鸿沟，为自动驾驶长尾泛化提供了一种可扩展的数据生成方案。

Abstract: Scaling robust driving policies is fundamentally bottlenecked by the scarcity of edge cases in curated datasets. While the real world continuously captures these critical events, such long-tail events remain underutilized when collected from heterogeneous sources. Specifically, diverse but valuable in-the-wild long-tail videos lack the full view coverage required for training policy models, often missing multi-view poses or originating solely from monocular dash cameras. This modality gap prevents these ubiquitous observations from being converted into scalable training data for long-tail generalization. We introduce OpenLongTail, an open-source generative data engine for scaling autonomous driving policies under long-tail events. To transform heterogeneous data sources into view-aligned and temporally coherent multi-view assets that are useful for policy learning, we develop a pose-informed extrapolative view synthesis pipeline that generates the missing views. We further enhance cross-view consistency and the temporal alignment for the newly generated views by injecting Plücker ray geometry into the scalable generation engine. By synthesizing heterogeneous long-tail data, we observe a significant improvement in closed-loop driving robustness in handling long-tail events. By measuring the extrapolative view synthesis and pose metrics, we validate the effectiveness of OpenLongTail in visual fidelity, cross-view consistency, and ego-trajectory recovery.

</details>


### [70] [PanoWorld: Real-World Panoramic Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09661)
*Haoyuan Li,Dizhe Zhang,Yuemei Zhou,Xiangkai Zhang,Haoran Feng,Xiaofan Lin,Wenjie Jiang,Bo Du,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PanoWorld，用于提升全景世界模型的长程记忆与物理一致性建模能力，并构建 World360 数据集进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全景世界模型在处理大尺度空间变化、长程依赖和复杂光照条件时存在不足；全景表示具有旋转等变性，可将旋转视为隐式几何变换，从而为简化轨迹建模和增强记忆提供机会。

Method: 作者提出 PanoWorld：通过固定航向将相机轨迹简化为平移建模；设计 Dense Panoramic Ray-Conditioning（DPRC）用于当前动作建模，设计 Geometry-aware Memory Augmentation（GMA）用于长程记忆增强；并采用三阶段训练流程逐步优化各模块。同时构建包含真实全景无人机视频与 AirSim360 高质量仿真视频的大规模数据集 World360。

Result: 在 World360 上的大量实验表明，PanoWorld 相比替代方法取得显著性能提升，尤其在大尺度空间变化和多样光照条件下表现更优。

Conclusion: PanoWorld 通过利用全景表示的旋转等变性和几何感知记忆机制，有效缓解了全景世界模型中的长程记忆难题；World360 为该方向提供了更具挑战性的评估基准。

Abstract: In this work, we aim to address the challenge of long-range memory in panoramic world models by exploiting the rotation-equivariant property of omnidirectional representations, where rotation can be treated as an implicit geometric transformation.Building on this insight, we propose PanoWorld, which simplifies camera trajectories into translations via fixed headings for both current-action modeling and long-range memory through Dense Panoramic Ray-Conditioning (DPRC) and Geometry-aware Memory Augmentation (GMA).Then, a three-stage training pipeline is introduced to progressively optimize each component. To better evaluate physical consistency under large-scale spatial variations and diverse illumination conditions, where existing datasets are relatively stable, we construct World360, a large-scale dataset consisting of both real-world video clips collected via panoramic unmanned aerial vehicles and high-quality simulated clips generated by AirSim360.Extensive experiments on World360 demonstrate the effectiveness of PanoWorld, outperforming alternative methods by a large margin.Our models, training code, and dataset will be publicly available. More information can be found on our project page: https://lihaoy-ux.github.io/panoworld-page/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [71] [HALO: Hybrid Adaptive Latent Reasoning for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08775)
*Micah Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HALO通过自适应地为部分token分配第二阶段潜在精炼，在冻结预训练语言模型上以更少计算获得优于固定精炼基线的整体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结预训练语言模型可通过额外精炼步骤提升性能，但固定精炼策略存在效率与效果矛盾：一步精炼可能能力不足，两步全序列精炼又可能带来冗余计算且迁移收益有限。

Method: 提出HALO，一种混合自适应潜在精炼方法：先进行粗粒度精炼，再基于token评分与单调token停止机制，仅对被选择的token执行第二阶段潜在精炼。

Result: 在由MMLU-Pro和GPQA-Diamond构成的主要公开基准比较中，HALO在论文报告方法中取得最佳总体平均表现，超过冻结backbone、fixed-1和fixed-2；内部分析显示其token准确率接近fixed-2，但平均精炼步数少于fixed-1且远少于fixed-2。

Conclusion: 性能提升的关键不在于简单增加精炼次数，而在于更合理地分配精炼计算；HALO同时实现了更强的公开基准结果和更低的控制器计算开销。

Abstract: We study how to improve a frozen pretrained language model with a small amount of adaptive extra computation. A simple approach is to add additional refinement steps on top of the backbone hidden states, but fixed extra refinement can be wasteful: a one-step refinement head may be too weak, while forcing a second full-sequence refinement step everywhere can increase compute without improving transfer. We introduce HALO, a hybrid adaptive latent-refinement method that combines a coarse refinement stage with selective second-stage latent refinement on a subset of tokens chosen by token scoring and monotonic token halting. On the main public benchmark comparison built from MMLU-Pro and GPQA-Diamond, HALO achieves the best overall average among the paper-facing methods, outperforming the frozen backbone, fixed-1, and fixed-2. Internal analysis further shows that HALO reaches nearly the same token-accuracy level as fixed-2 while using fewer average applied refine steps than fixed-1 and far fewer than fixed-2. These results suggest that the key advantage is not simply more refinement, but a better allocation of refinement: HALO achieves the strongest paper-facing result while also using less measured controller compute than either fixed baseline.

</details>


### [72] [An Emergent Mirage: Is Emergent Misalignment and Realignment Indeed a Robust Phenomenon?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09053)
*Abhinav Rao,Liancheng Gong,Bin Hu,Atharva Naik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统复现并检验“涌现性错位”（Emergent Misalignment, EM），发现其现象与所谓快速再对齐并不稳健，容易受到数据集表面特征（尤其回答长度差异）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究声称，模型在狭窄领域的错位数据上微调后，会突然表现出广泛错位行为，并且可通过少量再对齐快速逆转。本文旨在检验该现象是否稳健，以及相关机制证据是否可靠。

Method: 作者设计受控的反复对齐/错位微调循环，持续跟踪模型行为表现与训练过程中 LoRA 表征变化，并控制数据集表面因素（如回答长度）来重新评估错位、再对齐及机制特征。

Result: 研究复现了 EM，但发现错位与再对齐对浅层数据特征高度敏感；在控制回答长度差异后，表面上快速再对齐的现象大幅减弱或消失。同时，先前报告的 LoRA 表征空间相变等机制信号并不稳定地对应行为错位。

Conclusion: 当前关于 EM 的证据不如既有研究所声称的稳健。未来需要更严格的评测协议，控制数据集表面伪影，才能判断 EM 是否是真实且鲁棒的模型行为现象。

Abstract: Recent work has reported Emergent Misalignment (EM), where language models fine-tuned on narrow, domain-specific misaligned datasets abruptly acquire broadly misaligned behavior, alongside evidence that this behavior can be reversed through limited realignment. We systematically study repeated alignment and misalignment cycles using controlled fine-tuning loops while tracking behavioral performance, and LoRA representations throughout training. Although we reproduce EM, we find that both misalignment and realignment are highly sensitive to superficial dataset characteristics, with apparent rapid realignment largely disappearing after controlling for response-length differences.
