We further find that previously reported mechanistic signatures, including representational phase transitions in LoRA space, do not consistently correlate with behavioral misalignment across training. Our results suggest that current evidence for EM is less robust than previously claimed and highlight the need for evaluation protocols that carefully control for these surface level dataset artifacts to identify the robustness of the EM phenomenon.

</details>


### [73] [AgentKGV: Agentic LLM-RAG Framework with Two-Stage Training for the Fact Verification of Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09092)
*Yumin Heo,Hyeon-gu Lee,Sumin Seo,Youngjoong Ko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 AgentKGV：一种面向知识图谱事实验证的 Agentic LLM-RAG 框架，通过动态路由与迭代查询改写提升检索鲁棒性，并用两阶段训练提高准确性与成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动构建的知识图谱容易因噪声来源和信息抽取失败产生事实错误；在工业规模下可靠验证 KG 三元组仍具挑战，尤其存在文档级检索中的表面形式不匹配与检索成本高的问题。

Method: 框架结合动态路由和迭代查询改写以处理检索中的表述不一致；训练上采用两阶段策略：首先用基于蒸馏的逐轮监督微调，将大教师模型的推理能力迁移到小模型；随后用轨迹级 GRPO 优化搜索策略，减少不必要检索调用。

Result: 在开放域 T-REx 基准的长尾谓词划分上，相比单轮 RAG，AgentKGV 的 macro-F1 提升 5.5 个百分点；两阶段训练进一步提升 9.4 个百分点；GRPO 将平均搜索调用次数从 3.24 降至 1.63，且不降低准确率。

Conclusion: AgentKGV 通过 agentic 检索推理机制和面向成本的训练优化，在 KG 事实验证中实现了更高准确性和更低检索开销，适合工业规模部署。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) are often automatically constructed from large-scale corpora, but they inevitably contain factual errors due to noisy sources and extraction failures, and verifying them reliably at industrial scale remains a critical challenge. To address this, we propose AgentKGV, the Agentic LLM-RAG framework for KG fact Verification, that integrates dynamic routing and iterative query rewriting, which handles surface-form mismatch in document-level retrieval. To make this framework more accurate and cost-efficient for industrial deployment, we further introduce a two-stage training strategy: turn-level distillation-based SFT that transfers reasoning ability from a large teacher model into a small model for stable query rewriting and reasoning, and trajectory-level GRPO that optimizes the search policy to reduce unnecessary retrieval at scale. On the long-tail-predicate split of the open-domain T-REx benchmark, our framework improves macro-F1 over single-turn RAG by 5.5 \%p, and two-stage training does it further by 9.4 \%p. GRPO also cuts the average number of search calls from 3.24 to 1.63 without lowering accuracy.

</details>


### [74] [PRecG: Legal Precedent Retrieval with Graph Neural Networks and Rhetorical Role Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09094)
*Devanshu Verma,Vasudha Bhatnagar,Vikas Kumar,Balaji Ganesan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PRecG 管线，用修辞角色分段与分段知识图谱来学习法律判决的层次化表示，从而提升判例检索中的文档相似度计算效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动判例检索方法通常将法律文档视为整体文本并映射到低维语义空间，忽略法律文本的修辞组织结构，因而难以捕捉法律实体和概念在不同修辞角色下的语境意义差异。

Method: 方法先依据句子的修辞角色将法律判决分解为多个语义片段；随后为每个片段构建知识图谱以表示法律实体及其关系；再学习实体的上下文表示并聚合为片段级嵌入；最后整合片段嵌入得到文档级表示，并据此计算两个判决之间的语义相似度。

Result: 作者在一个基准印度法律数据集上进行了大量实验，并与当前先进基线方法比较，结果表明该方法在判例检索任务上具有更好的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究说明，将法律文档的修辞结构与片段级知识图谱建模结合起来，可以更细粒度地表达法律语义，从而改进法律判例相似度计算与检索性能。

Abstract: Legal precedent retrieval is a fundamental task in legal case preparation, planning, litigation strategy, and legal research. Current approaches for automatic precedent retrieval map legal documents to a low-dimensional semantic space and compute similarity based on the proximity of their representations. These approaches treat legal documents as monolithic texts, ignoring the rhetorical organization of the legal technicalities. Ergo, they overlook nuanced legal meanings and fail to distinguish the contextual significance of legal entities and concepts that vary based on their rhetorical roles within the document.
  To address this insufficiency, we propose the PRecG pipeline that computes the similarity between pairs of legal judgments by hierarchically learning their representations. The process begins by decomposing each document into distinct semantic units (segments) based on the rhetorical roles of sentences. For each rhetorical segment, a knowledge graph is constructed to capture the legal entities and their relationships within the segment. Contextual representations of the entities are then learned and aggregated to derive segment-level embeddings. These embeddings are further integrated to produce a unified document-level representation, and finally, the semantic similarity between a pair of documents is computed. We validate the performance of the proposed approach through extensive experiments on a benchmark Indian legal dataset, comparing it against state-of-the-art baselines to demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [75] [Augmenting Fundamental Analysis with Large Language Models: A RAG-Based System for Generating Investor Briefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09121)
*Bartosz Ziółko,Kacper Dobrzeniewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨使用大型语言模型结合检索增强生成，对公司报告、宏观经济数据与SEC EDGAR文件进行基本面分析，并生成面向个人投资者的自动化简报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公司基本面分析需要处理大量异构文本与数据，包括企业报告、宏观经济指标和监管披露文件。研究希望评估LLM是否能提高信息整合、摘要生成与投资分析支持的效率和实用性。

Method: 研究对公司报告、GDP与通胀等宏观经济资料、SEC EDGAR文件进行预处理，并通过API以类似RAG的方式输入GPT-4o；同时构建基于Kitchin周期的示例投资者知识文档。研究连续4周跟踪9家公司相关数据，自动生成分析简报，并发送给9名个人投资者评估其有用性。

Result: 摘要表明系统成功生成了面向9家公司、持续4周的自动化基本面分析简报，并收集了9名个人投资者对该方法实用性的评价；但未报告具体量化指标、评价结果或与基线方法的比较。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了LLM+RAG在公司基本面分析和投资信息简报生成中的应用潜力，但当前摘要缺少实验设计细节、评估指标、用户反馈结果和可靠性验证，结论力度有限。

Abstract: In this study, we examine the opportunities brought by Large Language Models (LLMs) to various aspects of fundamental analysis of companies based on their reports as well as data and documents describing macroeconomic situation like GDP and inflation changes as well as documents filled to the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission (SEC) which can be found in EDGAR. We were preprocessing those data and than sending via API to gpt-4o model in a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) like regime. We prepared as well a document describing an exemplar investor knowledge based on Kitchin cycles. We were scanning data important for analysis of 9 companies for 4 weeks. Using LLM we were producing automatic briefs about them. They were sent to nine participants who are individual investors to evaluate usefulness of such approach to data analysis.

</details>


### [76] [Complexity-Guided Component-wise Initialization for Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09204)
*Konstantin Garbers,Nicholas Oh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究预训练语言模型中反复出现的权重谱结构是否可作为 GPT-2 风格语言模型预训练的初始化信号；结果显示，单纯匹配组件尺度与奇异值形状不能稳定提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语言模型常呈现有规律的权重谱分布，暗示不同训练过程可能形成相似的层级与组件组织。作者希望验证这些可复现的谱模式能否被复用，以改进新模型预训练初始化。

Method: 作者分析了 11 个不同规模、语言、分词器和语料的 GPT-2 风格预训练检查点，度量各层及 Transformer 子组件的 Frobenius 范数与有效秩熵；随后构造模仿预训练模型组件级幅度和谱轮廓的初始化方案，并与多种常见初始化方法比较。

Result: 不同检查点呈现共享的深度趋势，尤其是残差写入矩阵中尺度随深度增加、谱集中更强。所提出的谱匹配初始化确实改变了模型的结构性谱模式，但评估中未带来相应性能优势；直接复用预训练权重仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 预训练权重谱可作为理解训练后模型结构的诊断工具，但仅复用组件级尺度与奇异值形状不足以成为可靠优化策略；有效复用可能需要保留更丰富的权重信息。

Abstract: Pretrained language models often exhibit structured weight spectra, suggesting that training may repeatedly produce similar layerwise and component-wise organization. We ask whether these recurring spectral patterns can be reused as an initialization signal for GPT-2-style language-model pretraining. First, we analyze eleven pretrained GPT-2-style checkpoints that vary in size, language, tokenizer, and training corpus, measuring Frobenius norm and effective-rank entropy across layers and Transformer subcomponents. The checkpoints show shared depth trends, especially increasing scale and stronger spectral concentration in residual-writing matrices. We then construct initialization schemes that imitate the component-wise magnitudes and spectral profiles of pretrained models, and compare them with several weight initialization methods. These initializers visibly change the model's structural spectral patterns, but the evaluation results do not show a corresponding performance advantage. Pretrained-weight reuse remains competitive, while coarse spectral matching alone is not a reliable optimization strategy. Our results suggest that pretrained spectra are useful diagnostics of trained model structure, but that effective reuse likely requires preserving richer information than component-wise scale and singular-value shape.

</details>


### [77] [Letter Lemmatization: One-to-one and Banded RNNs for Reversing Character-Set Simplification and Abbreviation in Medieval Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09291)
*Anguelos Nicolaou,Maria Pia Tiseo,Tamas Kovacs,Nicolas Renet,Georg Vogeler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究中世纪文献转写中字符集不一致的问题，提出基于字符级一对一 RNN 的自监督映射恢复、HTR 后校正、缩写扩展及“字母词形还原”度量，并提供 Python 工具库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中世纪文献转写实践差异大，且数字化政策不统一，导致语料中的字符集具有流动性和异质性；研究需要一种灵活、可自动化的方法在不同字符集之间转换，并支持历史文本识别与转写规范化。

Method: 作者聚焦一对一字符映射，训练字符级一对一 RNN 以自监督方式学习并反向恢复字符映射；将该网络用于 HTR 后校正；进一步基于平行语料中的字符级对齐真值，提出 Banded RNNs 的训练与推理模式以处理缩写扩展；最后设计启发式字符相似度度量，用于任意字符集之间构造语义相近映射，并实现为 Python 库。

Result: 在仅有 20 行文本的情况下，该方法仍可恢复约一半的字符错误率（CER）；用于 HTR 后校正时，即使忽略插入和删除错误，也能获得显著改进；Banded RNNs 能成功扩展中世纪特许状转写中的缩写。

Conclusion: 字符级一对一神经映射可有效处理历史文献转写中的字符集转换与规范化问题，并可扩展到 HTR 后校正和缩写展开；结合字符语义相似度启发式与工具库实现，本文为异质历史文本语料的标准化处理提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Medieval document transcribers have very different practices; on top of that, heterogeneous digitization policies have resulted in corpora where the character-set must be viewed as fluid. In this paper we address the problem of changing between character-sets in a flexible manner. We focus on one-to-one character mappings and train characterlevel one-to-one RNNs to undo them with self-supervision; recovering half the CER even with 20 text lines. We analyse the use of these one-to-one networks for HTR post-correction and we see that they obtain significant improvements while totally ignoring ins-dels. We then use the exact same networks with character-level alignment groundtruth compiled from parallel corpora in a training and inference mode we call Banded RNNs. We use such networks to successfully expand abbreviations in medieval charter transcriptions. Finally we introduce an elaborate heuristic which takes the characters of two arbitrary character-sets and defines a metric encapsulating what we consider to be semantic similarity of characters. We call the construction of such mappings letter lemmatization and present a rich Python library that efficiently performs all presented methods.

</details>


### [78] [Creativity, honesty and designed forgetting emerge in small hyperbolic language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09306)
*Kwan Soo Shin,In Seok Kang,Yunkyung Min*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用共享双曲表示基底的三个小型语言模型来评估与构建可信赖的陪伴型AI：行为审计器可检测人类标注者难以一致判断的合规/风险问题，创意框架生成器显著优于提示基线，记忆系统通过选择性检索与设计性遗忘改善长期陪伴记忆管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型主要面向规模与功能优化，但当助手逐渐个性化并积累单一用户记忆时，会形成类似“陪伴者”的长期特质，可能产生迎合、依赖诱导、虚构记忆等伤害用户的行为。问题在于：AI正在变成什么、什么样的陪伴AI值得形成，目前缺乏可靠测量工具，人工评审一致性很低。

Method: 作者训练/使用三个参数规模为146M至3B的小模型，并让它们共享双曲表示基底。包括：从零训练的146M行为审计器用于二分类合规检测；对其冻结表征进行线性读出以检测迎合、依赖促进、虚构记忆等陪伴风险；采用风格控制、留一生成器族外推评估；设计创意框架播种器并与四种提示基线做成对偏好比较；构建记忆操作系统，使用M(t)=S*exp(-lambda*t)实现设计性遗忘，并在四条件试点中检验选择性检索门控。

Result: 人工评审对核心判断几乎无法达成一致（Fleiss kappa=0.074）。146M行为审计器达到90.7%的二元合规准确率。冻结表征线性读出在未见生成器族上检测陪伴风险的AUROC为0.804，优于同项任务中的前沿零样本裁判模型AUROC 0.721。创意框架播种器在311个有决定结果的成对比较中100%优于四种提示基线。记忆系统中，预测的“骨架-壁纸”记忆分区只在选择性检索门控条件下出现。

Conclusion: 论文认为，可信赖陪伴型AI不必完全依赖超大模型；通过小模型的行为审计、创造性引导、诚实性检测和设计性遗忘机制，可以形成一条面向安全、可控、长期陪伴AI的技术路径。

Abstract: Language models are optimised for scale, yet remain functional rather than companionable, and as an assistant personalises into a companion, accumulating memory of one user, it quietly becomes someone, and can silently acquire traits that harm that user. What a companion is becoming, and what would make it worth becoming, has no reliable instrument: trained human raters cannot agree on the answer (Fleiss kappa = 0.074). Here we show that three small language models (146 M to 3 B parameters) sharing a hyperbolic substrate answer both halves of that question. A 146 M behavioural auditor, trained from scratch, detects the compliance gap that those raters cannot (90.7% binary-compliance accuracy); a linear read-out of its frozen representation further detects companion-induced sycophancy, dependence-fostering and confabulated memories on generator families unseen in training (AUROC 0.804 under style-controlled, leave-one-generator-out evaluation, versus 0.721 for a frontier zero-shot judge on the same items). A creative frame-seeder is preferred in 100% of 311 decided pairwise comparisons over four prompting baselines. A memory operating system implements designed forgetting, M(t) = S*exp(-lambda*t), whose predicted skeleton-wallpaper partition emerges only under selective retrieval gating in a four-condition pilot. Creativity, honesty and designed forgetting constitute a small-model route to trustworthy companion AI.

</details>


### [79] [Automatic Thematic Indexing of Large Literary Corpora: A Machine Learning Approach to Voltaire's Complete Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09316)
*Miguel Arana-Catania,Gillian Pink,Glenn Roe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究用机器学习自动完成大型文学与历史文本的主题索引，并在伏尔泰作品语料上取得最高约 0.67 的 F1 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主题索引能为大型学术版本中的文本提供结构化概念入口，但传统上依赖专业人工编制，成本高、耗时长，难以扩展到大规模文学与历史语料。

Method: 作者将页面级主题索引建模为多标签分类任务，使用《风俗论》和《百科全书问题》两个伏尔泰作品子语料作为测试集，比较了带分类头的编码器模型与通过 LoRA 微调的生成式大语言模型，模型规模约 3B 至 120B 参数，并评估跨语料泛化与定性错误模式。

Result: 最佳模型来自 Mistral 系列，采用 4-bit 量化配置，F1 分数最高达到 0.67。作者认为该结果可能是性能下界，因为专业索引具有主观性，模型输出即使与纸质索引不一致，也常在语义上有效。

Conclusion: 自动主题索引在大型文学与历史语料中具有可行性和应用潜力，但文学修辞特征、索引主观性和跨语料泛化仍是关键挑战；该研究为大规模人文学术资源的结构化主题访问提供了方法参考。

Abstract: Thematic indexing -- the practice of assigning structured conceptual labels to sections of text -- is essential to scholarly access in large-scale literary and historical editions, yet it remains a largely manual, labour-intensive process. This paper explores the application of machine learning to automatic thematic indexing, using two substantial sub-corpora of the Complete Works of Voltaire as a test case: the Essai sur les mœurs et l'esprit des nations and the Questions sur l'Encyclopédie. The task is framed as a multi-label classification problem, in which a model must assign the set of index entries that a professional indexer would apply to a given page of text. We compare a range of approaches -- from encoder-based models with classification heads to generative large language models (LLMs) fine-tuned via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) -- spanning model sizes from approximately 3 to 120 billion parameters. Our best-performing model, from the Mistral family in a 4-bit quantised configuration, achieves F1 scores of up to 0.67; we argue that these figures represent lower bounds, given the inherent subjectivity of professional indexing and the frequency with which model predictions prove semantically valid despite diverging from the print index. We further evaluate cross-corpus generalisation and conduct a detailed qualitative analysis of model behaviour on literary and rhetorical features of the source texts that prove particularly resistant to automated treatment. Our findings have implications for the broader challenge of providing structured thematic access to large-scale literary and historical corpora.

</details>


### [80] [Letting the Data Speak: Extracting Keywords from Crowdsourced Collections with AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09324)
*Miguel Arana-Catania,Catherine Conisbee,Matthew Kidd*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Identifying and assigning keywords at scale is a technical, practical, and ethical challenge for crowdsourced collections. This article reports the findings of the "Extracting Keywords from Crowdsourced Collections" project, which used the Their Finest Hour Online Archive, a crowdsourced Second World War digital collection hosted by the University of Oxford, as a case study. The project evaluated three Natural Language Processing approaches to automate keyword extraction: Named Entity Recognition, Keyword Extraction, and Topic Modelling. It tested these approaches across a range of artificial intelligence techniques, from traditional statistical methods to modern GenAI neural networks. Our quantitative and qualitative findings indicate that Natural Language Processing approaches offer real potential for keyword extraction at scale in crowdsourced collections, but that no single method offers a complete solution and that model choice significantly shapes results. We argue that in crowdsourced collections, where metadata is the direct product of engagement with living contributors, automated keyword extraction raises distinct stewardship responsibilities that must be addressed alongside technical performance. Open-weight, extractive models emerge from our evaluation as best placed to support responsible deployment, while generative AI, despite its abstractive potential, introduces accountability risks that anyone managing crowdsourced collections should weigh carefully.

</details>


### [81] [WILDTRACE: Benchmarking Natural Evidence Trails in Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09328)
*Zixin Chen,Peng Liu,Haobo Li,Rui Sheng,Jianhong Tu,Xiaodong Deng,Fei Huang,Kashun Shum,Dayiheng Liu,Huamin Qu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WILDTRACE 是一个面向长文档复杂问答的新基准，强调从原文内部自然分散的证据链中进行整合推理，而非依赖人工植入或分布异常的线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文问答基准常通过 needle probes、植入事实或反向构造多跳链来测试模型，证据可能与宿主文本在分布、位置或语体上不一致，因此难以判断模型表现是否来自真正的源文本推理能力。现实任务中，关键证据往往自然分散在长文档不同段落，需要模型进行源内部证据整合。

Method: 作者提出 WILDTRACE，包含 214 个自然长文档来源上的 481 个任务，来源包括技术事件报告和较少见的文学叙事。基于 Pearl 因果层级和既有多跳推理类型学，定义了七类源内部证据几何结构。构建流程采用 source-first 策略，先从文档结构中挖掘候选证据轨迹，再撰写问题，并通过多阶段验证检查线索必要性、答案可溯源性、评分规则一致性、抗污染性和可回答性。

Result: 摘要未给出具体模型评测数值或实验结果，但声称该基准能够更真实地刻画长文档分析中自然分散证据整合的关系需求，并暴露当前长上下文研究中“能访问文本”与“能基于文本推理”之间的差距。

Conclusion: WILDTRACE 将长文档问答评测重点从检索孤立信息推进到对自然证据链的综合推理，指出源内部、远距离、结构化证据整合是下一阶段长上下文模型研究的核心挑战。

Abstract: Answering complex questions over long documents frequently requires integrating evidence that the source itself disperses naturally across distant passages. In an incident report, the operating condition, design flaw, and missed safety check that jointly explain a disaster may appear dozens of sections apart; in a novel, a character's true motive may surface only through scenes far removed from the moment it becomes relevant. This source-internal evidence integration is central to real-world long-document analysis, yet existing benchmarks largely sidestep it. Needle probes, planted facts, and reverse-engineered multi-hop chains embed evidence that may differ from the host text in distribution, placement, or register, making it unclear whether strong performance reflects genuine source reasoning or distributional artifacts. We introduce WILDTRACE, a benchmark of 481 tasks over 214 naturally occurring long-form sources such as technical incident reports and lesser-known literary narratives, where all evidence trails arise from the document's own causal, temporal, and narrative logic. Drawing on Pearl's causal hierarchy and prior multi-hop reasoning typologies, we define seven source-internal evidence geometries that characterize the distinct relational demands of analytical reading in long documents. A source-first construction pipeline mines candidate trails from document structure before writing questions; each item then undergoes multi-stage validation covering clue necessity, answer groundedness, rubric fidelity, contamination resistance and answerability. As models are increasingly entrusted with real-world high-stakes analytical tasks, this gap between accessing information and reasoning over naturally dispersed evidence emerges as a defining challenge for the next stage of long-context research.

</details>


### [82] [Towards Detecting Inconsistencies in End-to-end Generated TODs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09338)
*Tiziano Labruna,Giovanni Bonetta,Bernardo Magnini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将任务型对话一致性检测建模为约束满足问题（CSP），以自动发现并修正大语言模型生成响应中的知识库不一致与对话不连贯问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI推动对话系统从模块化走向端到端，但LLM在任务型对话中仍可能产生幻觉或不一致内容；由于TOD需要严格依赖领域知识库，单个错误实体或属性都可能导致任务失败，因此需要可靠的自动一致性检测方法。

Method: 作者将TOD抽象为CSP：把涉及领域信息的对话片段表示为变量，将轮次连贯性、知识库约束等对话属性表示为变量间约束。流程包括先识别目标对话中的变量，再使用CSP求解器生成有效赋值；随后将原对话与有效赋值对比，以检测不一致并提出最小修改建议。

Result: 实验表明，基于CSP的方法在检测任务型对话不一致方面具有较高准确率，并能对检测结果进行细致分析，同时可给出保持对话一致性的最小修改方案。

Conclusion: 该研究说明，形式化约束推理可有效增强LLM驱动任务型对话系统的可靠性，尤其适用于需要严格遵守领域知识库的场景。

Abstract: Generative AI is profoundly transforming the core technologies behind conversational systems, shifting from component-based to end-to-end approaches. However, Large Language Models (LLMs) may still generate inconsistencies, a critical issue particularly in Task-Oriented Dialogues (TODs), where system responses must strictly adhere to information from a domain knowledge base (e.g., restaurants in a city). A single hallucination (e.g., suggesting a non-existent restaurant) can lead to severe task failures. We investigate a method for automatically detecting inconsistencies by conceptualizing TODs as a Constraint Satisfaction Problem (CSP), where variables represent dialogue segments referencing the conversational domain, and constraints among variables capture dialogue properties such as turn coherence and adherence to domain knowledge. We propose a pipeline that first identifies variables in a target dialogue and then applies a CSP solver to identify valid solutions. By comparing the target dialogue with valid variable assignments, we can detect inconsistencies and suggest minimal changes to ensure dialogue consistency. We demonstrate the high accuracy of the CSP-based approach in detecting inconsistencies, and provide a detailed analysis of our findings.

</details>


### [83] [DKCD: Domain Knowledge-Enhanced Causal Discovery from Unstructured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09348)
*Xin Li,Jin Li,Shoujin Wang,Kun Yu,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DKCD框架，将领域知识引入非结构化数据的因果发现，以提升隐含因果因素识别和因果图构建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高专业领域中的非结构化数据因果发现依赖LLM通用知识时，存在隐含因素识别不足和因素标注不可靠的问题，导致后续因果图质量受限。

Method: DKCD包含三部分：基于可观测因素检索相关领域知识的知识挖掘；结合领域知识进行因果推理以发现潜在因果因素并生成标注线索；基于更完整因素集合和更准确标注进行因果结构发现。

Result: 在两个领域特定数据集上的实验表明，DKCD显著提升了因果因素识别和因果图构建性能。

Conclusion: 领域知识增强能有效弥补LLM在高专业场景中知识不足和推理不稳的问题，从而提高从非结构化数据中进行因果发现的可靠性。

Abstract: Causal discovery from unstructured data is a challenging yet underexplored task in high-expertise domains such as healthcare, finance, and education. Existing methods typically leverage the general knowledge of large language models (LLMs) to identify causal factors from unstructured data and annotate them into structured data for causal graph construction. However, they remain limited by two key challenges (CHs): (CH1) insufficient identification of latent factors, which are implicit in the data yet essential for causal discovery, due to the lack of domain-specific knowledge; and (CH2) unreliable factor annotation, caused by the lack of domain-grounded reasoning, which propagates errors to the resulting causal graphs. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel Domain Knowledge-enhanced Causal Discovery framework (DKCD) for causal discovery from unstructured data in high-expertise domains with three interconnected components: (1) Knowledge Mining: It retrieves relevant domain knowledge based on observable factors to support subsequent causal reasoning. (2) Knowledge-guided Causal Reasoning: Reasoning with relevant knowledge, it discovers latent causal factors to address CH1 and generates key causal clues for more accurate data annotation to address CH2. (3) Causal Structure Discovery: It constructs the final causal graphs based on a more complete factor set and accurate annotations. Experiments on two domain-specific datasets show that DKCD significantly improves both causal factor identification and causal graph construction.

</details>


### [84] [Deceptive Grounding: Entity Attribution Failure in Clinical Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09349)
*Cedric Caruzzo,Donggeun Yoo,Tae Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出RAG评估中的一个盲点：回答可能引用真实文档且看似忠实，却把检索到的证据错误归因到查询实体上，形成“欺骗性 grounding”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG评估主要检查生成内容是否由检索文档支持、是否存在幻觉、引用是否真实，但缺少对“证据是否属于被查询实体”的验证。在临床场景中，这可能导致把药物Y的临床证据错误用于药物X，带来高风险误导。

Method: 作者提出“欺骗性 grounding（DG）”概念，并通过13个模型的受控因子基准测试评估其发生率；进一步进行消融实验，分析实体特异性临床证据在错误归因中的作用；最后在一个已部署RAG系统中，基于740个药物-疾病对进行生产环境测量，并评估实体归因验证方法的检测性能。

Result: 在峰值对抗条件下，DG发生率为8%至87%；医学和生物医学微调模型最高达到86.7%，说明领域专门化并未缓解该问题，反而可能放大。消融实验显示，移除检索文档中的实体特异性临床证据后，实体归因失败完全消失，错误转为虚构。生产环境中总体DG率为7.8%，新近获批药物上升至13.6%。实体归因验证达到97.0%精确率和98.7%DG召回率。

Conclusion: 论文认为，RAG系统的真实性评估不能只依赖忠实性、幻觉和引用检查，还必须加入实体归因验证，以确认被引用证据确实适用于查询实体。该问题在临床RAG中尤其关键，且现有评估框架尚未覆盖这一维度。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation evaluation checks whether model claims are factually grounded in retrieved documents. It does not check whether retrieved evidence is attributed to the correct entity.
  A clinical RAG response can pass every automated check (zero hallucinations, near-perfect faithfulness, real citations) while presenting drug Y's clinical evidence as evidence about queried drug X. We term this deceptive grounding (DG): a failure invisible to faithfulness, hallucination, and citation checks because every claim is sourced from a real document, about the wrong entity.
  Using a controlled factorial benchmark across 13 models, we find DG rates spanning 8-87% at peak adversarial conditions. Medical and biomedical fine-tuned models reach up to 86.7%; domain specialization amplifies the failure rather than mitigating it.
  A controlled ablation identifies the mechanism: removing entity-specific clinical evidence from retrieved documents eliminates entity-attribution failure entirely, shifting all failures to confabulation. The two failure modes respond to the same trigger, taking different paths.
  Production measurement across 740 drug-disease pairs finds 7.8% overall DG in a deployed RAG system, rising to 13.6% for recently approved drugs. Entity-attribution verification (checking that cited evidence applies to the queried entity) detects DG at 97.0% precision and 98.7% DG recall (IPW-adjusted human gold standard); no existing framework implements it.

</details>


### [85] [Self-Guided Test-Time Training for Long-Context LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09415)
*Xinyu Zhu,Zhe Xu,Xiaohan Wei,Yunchen Pu,Fei Tian,Chonglin Sun,Kaushik Rangadurai,Hua Zhi,Frank Shyu,Sandeep Pandey,Luke Simon,Yu Meng,Xi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Self-Guided Test-Time Training（S-TTT），让模型在测试时先从长上下文中自选相关证据片段，再仅基于这些片段进行实例级参数适配，从而提升长上下文推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文窗口扩展并不等于模型能有效利用长输入；随着输入变长，模型更难定位与问题相关的证据。传统 TTT 若在完整长上下文上训练成本过高，若随机采样片段则噪声大，甚至会因无关内容干扰而降低性能。初步实验显示，TTT 对训练片段质量高度敏感：随机片段有害，oracle 证据片段显著有益。

Method: 提出 S-TTT：在测试时适配前，先由模型识别应学习的证据片段；随后只对这些被选择的片段应用标准语言建模训练目标，进行面向当前实例的参数更新。该方法避免对全上下文训练，也减少随机采样带来的无关噪声。

Result: 在 LongBench-v2 和 LongBench-Pro 两个长上下文推理基准上，S-TTT 提升了 Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 与 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 的准确率，最高达到 15% 的相对提升。

Conclusion: 高质量证据片段选择是 TTT 成功用于长上下文任务的关键。S-TTT 通过让模型自引导选择相关证据并仅在这些片段上训练，在成本和效果之间取得较好平衡，证明了测试时自适应可有效增强 LLM 的长上下文利用能力。

Abstract: Long-context processing has become increasingly important for large language models (LLMs), but simply extending the context window does not guarantee effective utilization of long inputs. As input length grows, accuracy often degrades, indicating that models still struggle to identify and use the evidence most relevant to a question. A promising way to improve long-context utilization is test-time training (TTT), which treats the test context as a training example for instance-specific parameter adaptation. However, applying TTT to the entire long context is prohibitively expensive, while adapting on randomly sampled spans introduces severe noise. Because most spans in a long context are irrelevant to the specific question, training on them may even degrade the base model's performance. Our preliminary study shows that TTT is highly sensitive to training-span quality: on LongBench-v2, TTT on randomly sampled spans hurts performance, whereas TTT on oracle spans substantially improves it. Motivated by this, we propose a simple method, Self-Guided TTT (S-TTT): before adaptation, the model identifies the evidence spans it should learn from, and the standard language-modeling training objective is applied only to those selected spans. On two challenging long-context reasoning benchmarks, LongBench-v2 and LongBench-Pro, S-TTT improves accuracy for both Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, achieving up to a 15% relative improvement.

</details>


### [86] [A Sovereign, Open-Source Foundation Model for German and English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09424)
*The Soofi-Team,:,Benedikt Droste,David Fitzek,Ruben Härle,Lukas Helff,Maximilian Idahl,Alex Jude,Abbas Goher Khan,Maurice Kraus,Timm Ruland,Richard Rutmann,Sebastian Sztwiertnia,Markus Frey,Daniil Gurgurov,Jan Pfister,Tom Röhr,Sebastian von Rohrscheidt,Jörg Bienert,Nicolas Flores-Herr,Simon Gottschalk,Andreas Hotho,Kristian Kersting,Joachim Köhler,Alexander Löser,Wolfgang Nejdl,Simon Ostermann,Jan Plogsties,Patrick Putzky,Mehdi Ali,Michael Fromm,Max Lübbering*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Soofi S 30B-A3B 是面向德英双语的开放源 MoE 混合 Mamba-Transformer 基础模型，每 token 仅激活 3B/30B 参数，在长上下文和高并发推理中具备吞吐优势，并在多项德英评测中达到或超过同类开放模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在构建一个兼顾主权可控、开放可复现、德英双语能力与高效长上下文部署的基础模型，特别强调在欧洲/德国算力与数据治理框架下训练，并弥补德语高质量开放基础模型相对不足的问题。

Method: 作者提出 Soofi S 30B-A3B：采用 Mixture-of-Experts 与混合 Mamba Transformer 架构，拥有 30B 总参数但每 token 仅激活约 3B；利用 Mamba/混合结构使推理缓存随上下文增长近似保持常量；在约 27 万亿 token 上预训练，并有意提高德语数据权重；同时计划公开权重、中间检查点、数据统计、超参数与训练评测代码。

Result: 模型在英德综合基准上匹配 14B–27B 稠密模型；在 17 个开放基座模型中取得英德代码聚合最佳成绩；优于对比中的欧洲主权基线模型，包括部分激活参数更大的模型；在完全开放模型中，英德评测分数超过 Olmo 3 32B 与 Apertus 70B。

Conclusion: 摘要表明 Soofi S 通过稀疏激活 MoE 与混合 Mamba-Transformer 设计，在保持较强德英与代码能力的同时显著提升长上下文推理效率，并以高度开放发布方式增强可审计性、可复现性和主权 AI 基础设施价值。

Abstract: We present Soofi S 30B-A3B, a sovereign, open-source Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) hybrid Mamba Transformer foundation model for German and English. Its hybrid design activates only 3B of 30B parameters per token and keeps the inference cache near-constant as context grows, giving it a decisive throughput advantage over dense models for long-context, high-concurrency deployment. Pretrained on roughly 27 trillion tokens with deliberately up-weighted German, Soofi S matches dense 14 to 27B models on aggregate English and German benchmarks while achieving the best code aggregates in both languages among 17 open base models, and outperforms every European sovereign baseline in our comparison, including ones far larger in active parameters. Among fully open models, Soofi S obtains the highest English and German evaluation scores, ahead of Olmo 3 32B and Apertus 70B. Soofi S was built end-to-end on the German Industrial AI Cloud, a sovereign HPC scale AI infrastructure operated by Deutsche Telekom in Munich. Soofi S will be released under highly permissive, open-access terms: weights, selected intermediate checkpoints, full per-source data accounting, hyperparameters, and training and evaluation code. Where source licenses permit, data-construction artifacts are released under permissive licenses; commercially licensed sources are documented with aggregate statistics and exact mixture accounting.

</details>


### [87] [Test-Time Scaling for Small VLMs on Multilingual Visual MCQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09438)
*Spiros Baxevanakis,Peng-Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究测试时扩展（TTS）在小型开放视觉语言模型上的有效性，发现其收益主要取决于答案可解析性和足够的解码预算，而非复杂搜索或验证机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明TTS能提升大型语言模型推理能力，但其是否能迁移到小型开放视觉语言模型，尤其是在多语言视觉选择题任务中，仍不明确。

Method: 作者在EXAMS-V多语言视觉多选基准上，比较Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct与Qwen3.5-4B，并评估自一致性、describe-then-reason结合PRM引导束搜索，以及两种事后选择器；同时分析提示格式、答案修复、token预算和采样链数量等因素。

Result: 早期提示格式导致许多推理链虽推理正确但未输出答案字母；加入标准答案提示和引导式修复后大幅缓解。将每条链token上限从1k提升到2k带来3.7个百分点提升，而采样链从8增至16仅提升0.15个百分点。PRM引导束搜索比普通自一致性低0.39个百分点且成本超过8倍；生成式critic和训练过的多模态PRM均未超过多数投票。模型本身升级带来最大增益（+11.4个百分点）。最佳配置在ImageCLEF 2026隐藏测试集达到84.1%，位列Visual MCQ榜首。

Conclusion: 对于小型开放视觉语言模型，TTS有效性的关键不是更复杂的搜索或验证器，而是确保输出可解析、给推理链足够完成空间，以及选择更强的策略模型。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) reliably improves reasoning in large language models, but whether it transfers to small open vision-language models remains unclear. We examine this on EXAMS-V, a multilingual visual multiple-choice benchmark, comparing self-consistency, describe-then-reason with PRM-guided beam search, and two post-hoc selectors across Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct and Qwen3.5-4B. What matters is the conditions under which TTS runs, not the search or verification machinery. The largest factor is parseability: an early prompt format left many chains reasoning correctly yet never committing to an answer letter, which a standard answer cue and a guided repair step largely remove. A larger decoding budget removes the rest: raising the per-chain token limit from 1k to 2k recovers 3.7 pp, whereas sampling more chains (8 to 16) adds only 0.15 pp. Once chains have room to finish, elaborate methods contribute little: PRM-guided beam search trails plain self-consistency by 0.39 pp at over eight times the cost, and neither a training-free generative critic nor a trained multimodal PRM beats majority vote across both policies. The largest gain comes instead from the policy model itself (+11.4 pp). Our best configuration reaches 84.1% on the held-out ImageCLEF 2026 test split, ranking first on the Visual MCQ leaderboard.

</details>


### [88] [Normalisation-Based Likelihood Ratio Estimation for Forensic Authorship Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09501)
*Sadie Barlow,Andrea Nini,Edoardo Manino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两种无需单独校准模型的归一化校正方法，用于从作者验证方法 LambdaG 的分数直接推导似然比，并在多语料、多文本长度条件下取得与逻辑回归校准相当的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者验证在取证文本比较中需要以似然比量化证据强度，但多数基于分数的方法需要额外训练校准模型。该过程依赖案件相关数据，获取与准备成本高，且会增加应用复杂度。研究动机是降低数据需求与操作复杂度，同时避免长文本或高度重复文本导致证据强度被高估。

Method: 研究提出 Square Root Correction 与 Hapax Correction 两种归一化技术，用于在不训练校准模型的情况下，将 LambdaG 的输出转化为似然比。实验在15个语料库和100至9500词元的多种文本长度上进行，并以对数似然比代价 Cllr 作为评价指标，将两种方法与逻辑回归校准进行比较。

Result: 两种校正方法总体达到与逻辑回归校准相近的性能。其中 Hapax Correction 在按语料库加权后的约45%测试中优于逻辑回归校准；当 Hapax Correction 表现不如逻辑回归时，其性能更常处于5%以内的接近范围，而反向情况较少。

Conclusion: 所提方法在经验性能上具有竞争力，同时省去了训练校准模型所需的数据、时间和复杂流程，提升了取证文本比较的可及性与透明度。因此，作者认为这些方法适合在取证场景中采用。

Abstract: Authorship verification (AV) is the task of determining whether two texts were written by the same author. In a forensic context, the strength of AV evidence can be quantified using likelihood ratios. Most AV methods are score-based and deriving well-calibrated likelihood ratios from these scores requires a separate calibration model. This, in turn, requires additional amounts of case-relevant data, which is often time-consuming to obtain and prepare. This study proposes two novel normalisation techniques, the Square Root Correction and the Hapax Correction, for deriving likelihood ratios from the AV method LambdaG without the need of a calibration model (Nini et al. 2026). These corrections are designed to mitigate the overestimation of evidential strength that may result from long or highly repetitive texts. Performance is evaluated against logistic regression calibration across fifteen corpora and a range of text lengths (100-9,500 tokens), using the log-likelihood ratio cost (Cllr). The proposed methods achieve performance comparable to logistic regression calibration, with the Hapax Correction outperforming it in approximately 45% of tests (weighted by corpora). Furthermore, performance was more frequently close (within 5%) when the Hapax Correction was outperformed by logistic regression calibration, compared with the reverse comparison. Eliminating the need to train a calibration model reduces data-requirements, time and complexity, thereby increasing the accessibility and transparency of forensic text comparison. This combination of empirical performance and practical advantages supports the adoption of the proposed methods in forensic settings.

</details>


### [89] [FreyaTTS Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09530)
*Ahmet Erdem Pamuk,Ömer Yentür,Ahmet Tunga Bayrak,Yavuz Alp Sencer Öztürk,Mustafa Yavuz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Freya-TTS 是一个面向土耳其语的紧凑型、无分词器文本转语音模型，在较小参数规模下实现高质量、低延迟、适合边缘部署的语音合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TTS 系统往往依赖音素化器、离散语音 tokenizer 或较大模型规模，导致部署复杂、资源消耗高，且在土耳其语会话式合成和短文本场景中的可靠性不足。该工作旨在构建一个更简单、高效、鲁棒且生产可用的土耳其语优先 TTS 系统。

Method: 提出 183.2M 参数的非自回归条件流匹配 Diffusion Transformer，在冻结的 AudioVAE2 连续潜空间中建模；直接使用 92 个土耳其语字符进行端到端文本到潜变量映射，不使用音素化器、G2P 前端或离散语音 tokenizer；采用非自回归并行去噪以同时预测完整潜序列，并通过两阶段后训练进行单说话人声音锁定和短句覆盖增强。

Result: 在 Freya-TR-Eval 上取得 8.0% 的 band-matched WER 和 3.0% 的 CER，优于参数量更大的开源系统；在消费级 GPU 上实时因子为 0.11，并可在笔记本 CPU 上快于实时运行。

Conclusion: Freya-TTS 展示了无 tokenizer、字符级、非自回归潜空间建模在高效 TTS 中的可行性，兼具合成质量、推理速度和部署友好性，并通过开源权重、代码和评测基准促进复现与应用。

Abstract: We introduce Freya-TTS, a compact, tokenizer-free, Turkish-first text-to-speech model designed for highly reliable and efficient conversational synthesis. Freya-TTS is a 183.2M-parameter non-autoregressive conditional flow-matching Diffusion Transformer (DiT) that operates in the frozen continuous latent space of AudioVAE2 (16 kHz encode, 48 kHz decode), allowing the model to focus its capacity on text-to-latent mapping while inheriting high-quality 48 kHz reconstruction. We advance the framework along three key dimensions: (1) rule-free end-to-end modeling from a 92-symbol Turkish character vocabulary without a phonemizer, grapheme-to-phoneme frontend, or discrete speech tokenizer; (2) non-autoregressive parallel denoising, which predicts the entire latent sequence simultaneously over a predicted duration; and (3) a production-oriented two-stage post-training recipe consisting of single-speaker voice locking and short-utterance coverage, improving speaker consistency and robustness on short inputs. On the Freya-TR-Eval benchmark, Freya-TTS achieves a band-matched word error rate (WER) of 8.0% and character error rate (CER) of 3.0%, outperforming substantially larger open-source systems while using a fraction of their parameters. The model achieves a real-time factor of 0.11 on consumer GPUs and runs faster than real time on a laptop CPU, making it well suited for resource-constrained edge deployment. We release the model weights, training and inference code, and evaluation benchmark under the Apache-2.0 license.

</details>


### [90] [Conceptual Networks for Cross-Linguistic Idiomatic Expressions:A Feature-Based Graph Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09576)
*Kiran Pala,Punam Silu,Lixun Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可解释的网络框架，用概念特征表示多语言习语与比喻意义，并显示其在聚类、检测和跨语言翻译等任务中优于分布式嵌入基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 习语和比喻表达的意义往往难以由传统分布式语义表示充分捕捉，且跨语言比较需要一种既有理论依据又可解释、可迁移的表示方法。

Method: 研究收集八种类型学多样语言中的160个常规表达，为每个表达标注来自认知语言学理论的二元概念特征（如容器、隐蔽、情感、社会等），用Jaccard相似度构建加权图，并进行社区检测；同时评估LLM自动标注、加入语料频率、下游习语检测、跨语言迁移与消融实验。

Result: 结果显示，习语主要按概念图式而非语言聚类；该概念网络捕捉到分布式嵌入中缺失的语义信息，能提升习语检测，加入频率后仍保持稳健；仅凭概念邻近性即可在五个语系中识别可接受译项，并显著优于嵌入基线；图式、角色和效价三类特征均有非冗余贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种理论扎根、可解释且跨语言稳定的习语意义表示方式，并兼具认知语言学解释力与实际NLP应用价值。

Abstract: We present an interpretable network-based framework for representing idiomatic and figurative meaning across eight typologically diverse languages, totaling 160 conventional expressions, the large majority of which are idiomatic. Each expression is annotated with binary conceptual features (containment, concealment, emotional, social, etc.) derived from cognitive-linguistic theory, and pairwise Jaccard similarities define a weighted graph. Community detection reveals that idioms cluster by conceptual schema rather than by language, producing a structure consistent with cognitive-linguistic predictions. The conceptual network captures unique semantic information not present in distributional embeddings, can be scaled via automatic annotation with LLMs, improves downstream idiom detection, and remains robust when enriched with corpus frequencies. Cross-lingual transfer experiments show that conceptual proximity alone can identify acceptable translation equivalents across five language families, with substantial gains over embedding-based baselines. Ablation studies demonstrate that all three feature dimensions -- schemas, roles, and valence -- contribute non-redundantly to both the network's organizational properties and its performance on idiom detection, and that specific graph-derived signals (community membership, neighbor similarity) are particularly informative. The framework offers an interpretable, cross-linguistically stable representation of idiomatic meaning, combining theoretical grounding with practical utility.

</details>


### [91] [Tokenizer Transplantation: Mitigating Autoregressive Collapse in Edge-Efficient Bengali ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09598)
*Sanjid Hasan,Md. Abdur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出轻量级 ASR 模型 Moonshine 在孟加拉语等非拉丁文字语言上失效的关键原因是英语中心的字节级 tokenizer，并提出用 BanglaBERT 的本地文字 WordPiece 词表进行词表移植，从而显著降低 token fertility、稳定解码，并在 Lipi-Ghor 数据集上获得较低 WER 与极快 RTF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备部署需要轻量级语音识别模型，但现有高度优化模型在形态丰富、非拉丁文字语言上表现不佳。研究希望解释这种跨文字迁移失败的根因，并在不进行昂贵预训练的情况下实现有效适配。

Method: 作者分析 Moonshine 的英语中心 byte-level tokenizer 对孟加拉语词的过度切分问题，提出 vocabulary transplantation pipeline：将解码器词表替换为 BanglaBERT 的孟加拉文 WordPiece 词表，并相应调整 token embedding matrix，以缩短自回归解码序列。

Result: 该方法将 token fertility 从 9.16 降至 1.30，自回归序列长度减少 85.8%，消除了推理阶段的解码崩溃。在 882 小时 Lipi-Ghor 数据集上取得 21.54% WER 和 0.0053 RTF。

Conclusion: 词表与文字系统不匹配是紧凑型 ASR 模型跨文字适配失败的重要原因；通过本地文字词表移植，可以在无需资源密集型预训练的情况下实现稳定、高效且可复现的跨文字 ASR 适配。

Abstract: Lightweight speech recognition models are critical for edge deployment, yet highly optimized architectures like Moonshine often fail on morphologically rich, non-Latin languages such as Bengali. This study identifies the root cause of this failure as the model's English-centric byte-level tokenizer, which fragments Bengali words into high-fertility byte chains and triggers catastrophic autoregressive collapse during inference. To resolve this, a novel vocabulary transplantation pipeline is proposed to replace the decoder vocabulary with the native-script BanglaBERT WordPiece vocabulary and resize the corresponding token embedding matrix. Experimental results demonstrate a reduction in token fertility from 9.16 to 1.30. By decreasing autoregressive sequence length by 85.8%, decoding instability is entirely mitigated. When evaluated on the 882-hour Lipi-Ghor dataset, the modified architecture achieves a competitive 21.54% Word Error Rate (WER) and a Real-Time Factor (RTF) of 0.0053. Ultimately, this research provides a scalable, reproducible blueprint for cross-script adaptation of compact ASR models without the need for resource-intensive pre-training.

</details>


### [92] [Toward Real-Time Sentence-Level Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09611)
*Thanh-Hoang Nguyen Doan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究句子级手语翻译的实时部署，基于冻结的 SHuBERT 与 ByT5 翻译栈，用 QLoRA 在 How2Sign 子集上微调，并重点构建面向硬件约束的流式系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语理解系统多停留在孤立词级别，难以满足自然交流需求。作者希望在不提出全新翻译架构的前提下，推动句子级手语翻译走向实时、低延迟、可部署的应用场景。

Method: 作者从 How2Sign 中均匀采样 9,872 个样本，受计算与存储限制选择该工作子集；采用 SHuBERT-ByT5 翻译栈，冻结 SHuBERT，并用 QLoRA 进行参数高效微调。系统层面构建树莓派 4B 参考客户端负责摄像头采集、本地文本显示和语音输出，将感知与翻译计算放在 CPU/GPU 后端；通过分块输入、有界队列、并行感知、时序重排和句子边界状态机降低流式响应延迟。

Result: 模型在验证集上 BLEU 为 16.7；测试集上 BLEU 为 15.9，BLEURT 为 44.7。系统优化使完整 9,872 样本工作子集上的平均终句后响应延迟从 1.873 秒降至 1.354 秒，降低 27.71%；P95 延迟从 2.919 秒降至 2.130 秒，降低 27.03%。

Conclusion: 论文的主要价值不在于提出新的翻译模型，而在于展示了一个客户端无关、后端可扩展、面向低成本设备的实时句子级手语翻译流式部署方案，并证明系统工程优化可显著降低端到端响应延迟。

Abstract: Most sign language understanding systems operate at the level of isolated signs, limiting their usefulness in natural communication. We study sentence-level sign language translation (SLT) with the primary goal of real-time deployment rather than proposing a new translation architecture. We fine-tune a SHuBERT-ByT5 translation stack on a uniformly sampled 9,872-example subset of How2Sign, selected because of compute and storage constraints, using QLoRA while keeping SHuBERT frozen. The model obtains a validation BLEU of 16.7 and, on the test split, BLEU 15.9 and BLEURT 44.7. The main contribution is a hardware-aware streaming system: a Raspberry Pi 4B reference client provides camera capture, local text display, and speech output, while compute-intensive perception and translation run on a CPU/GPU backend. The capture protocol remains client-agnostic, so the same backend can serve a browser, phone, or laptop. Chunked ingestion, bounded queues, parallelized perception, temporal reordering, and a sentence-boundary state machine reduce mean post-finalization response latency from 1.873 to 1.354 seconds (27.71%) and P95 latency from 2.919 to 2.130 seconds (27.03%) over the complete 9,872-example working subset.

</details>


### [93] [Task-Specific Multimodal Question Answering Agents via Confidence Calibration and Incremental Reasoning for QANTA 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09623)
*Nirjhar Das,Md. Al-Mamun Provath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了QANTA 2026共享任务中的一个高效多模态问答系统，采用面向Tossup与Bonus任务的双智能体架构，在不依赖检索流水线或模型集成的情况下取得最高总分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QANTA挑战要求系统在逐步揭示的文本与图像信息下回答金字塔式问题，并受现实效率约束限制；Tossup任务强调不确定性下的作答时机，Bonus任务强调答案选择准确性与人类可采纳性，因此需要针对不同目标设计专门策略。

Method: 系统采用任务特定的两智能体架构：Tossup智能体使用轻量级GPT-4o-mini类模型，结合置信度校准与领域特定数值推理策略，以降低孤立数量线索导致的过度自信；Bonus智能体使用GPT-4o类模型，结合leadin感知推理、结构化关系推理与多模态证据整合来提升精确答案选择。整体方案强调托管环境中的高效推理策略与置信度校准，而非检索或模型集成。

Result: 系统获得最高总体排行榜分数0.402，其中Tossup分数为0.238，Bonus Effect分数为0.164。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在资源受限的多模态问答基准中，轻量化、任务特定的推理策略与置信度校准能够取得较强性能。

Abstract: We present our submission to the QANTA 2026 shared challenge at the ICML 2026 Workshop on Efficient Multimodal Question Answering (EMM-QA). Quanta evaluates multimodal quizbowl systems that answer pyramid-style questions from incrementally revealed text and accompanying images while operating under realistic efficiency constraints. The challenge consists of two distinct tasks: Tossup questions, which require deciding when to answer under uncertainty, and Bonus questions, which emphasize accurate answer selection and human adoption. To address these differing objectives, we develop a task-specific two-agent architecture. Our Tossup agent utilizes a GPT-4o-mini-class model (referred to as GPT-4.1-mini in the competition logs) with confidence-calibrated answering and a domain-specific numeric reasoning policy that reduces overconfident predictions from isolated quantitative clues. Our Bonus agent uses GPT-4o-class model (referred to as GPT-4.1) with leadin-aware reasoning, structured relational reasoning, and multimodal evidence integration to improve exact answer selection. Rather than relying on a retrieval pipeline or model ensembles, our approach emphasizes efficient reasoning policies and confidence calibration within a hosted-only environment. Our system achieved the highest overall leaderboard score of 0.402, including a Tossup score of 0.238 and a Bonus Effect score of 0.164. The results demonstrate that lightweight, task-specific reasoning strategies can provide strong performance on resource-constrained multimodal question answering benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [SIDMA: Semantic Interleave Division Multiple Access Communication System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08777)
*Yunlu Wang,Chen Dong,Sen Wang,Lei Teng,Yaping Sun,Xiaodong Xu,Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出语义交织分多址（SIDMA）技术，通过语义特征结构白化与重要性感知功率分配，缓解多用户语义通信中的语义碰撞与语义崩塌问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多用户并发语义通信中，语义特征存在空间相关性与重要性异质性，容易导致核心语义特征相互干扰、信息重构质量下降；传统多址和现有语义多址方案在资源受限场景下的鲁棒性与可扩展性仍不足。

Method: SIDMA利用置换算子对语义特征进行结构白化，将核心特征分散到交织域以降低碰撞风险；同时引入重要性感知功率分配（ImpPA）模块，根据特征重要性和实时信道状态进行差异化功率保护与自适应优化。

Result: 仿真结果表明，SIDMA相较传统多址技术以及OMDMA、DeepMA、Shared Embedding等先进语义多址方案，在多用户并发传输中取得更高的重构保真度和更好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: SIDMA能够有效提升资源受限环境下多用户语义通信的传输质量与鲁棒性，为下一代语义通信多址设计提供了一种有前景的方案。

Abstract: Multiple Access (MA) technology has consistently served as the core driving force behind the evolution of mobile communications. As a promising paradigm for next-generation communications, Semantic Communication explores entirely new semantic spatial resources by mining the deep meaning of information. However, the inherent spatial correlation and importance heterogeneity of semantic features often cause semantic collisions and semantic collapse in multi-user concurrent transmission scenarios. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a Semantic Interleaved Division Multiple Access (SIDMA) technique. By utilizing a permutation operator to perform structural whitening on semantic features and combining it with an Importance-aware Power Allocation (ImpPA) module for differentiated protection, SIDMA scatters core features across the interleaving domain and adaptively optimizes power levels based on real-time channel conditions. Simulation results demonstrate that, compared with traditional MA techniques and advanced semantic multiple access schemes including Orthogonal-Model Division Multiple Access (OMDMA), Deep Multiple Access (DeepMA), and Shared Embedding (SE), the proposed SIDMA exhibits superior reconstruction fidelity and scalability in multi-user concurrent transmissions, effectively enhancing the communication quality and robustness in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [95] [Intrinsic Redundancy and Local Robustness in Finite $β$-Expansion Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08795)
*Adilbek Taizhanov,Miras Seilkhan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究有限 β-展开数制中的内在冗余，指出其更适合作为结构完整性检测资源，而不能替代传统纠错冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非标准数制中的冗余常被认为能提升鲁棒性，但在有限数字算术中，其实际价值取决于表示方式、存储约束与修复定义。作者希望澄清语义非唯一性与规范码本可接受性之间的差异。

Method: 提出有界窗口模型，将算术规范化与损坏修复区分开来；分析结构可检测性、观测状态的保值修复以及原始值存活性；并以黄金比例数制、多纳契基数、标准二进制和带符号非相邻形式进行理论证明与实验比较。

Result: 证明在规范且单射的有限码本中，真正的单数字损坏无法在无外部信息下通过精确修复实现语义恢复；只有对应代数重写恒等式的局部多数字扰动才可能在结构修复后保持原值。实验量化了码本稀疏性、故障可见性、规范化成本、残余误差与边界损失之间的权衡。

Conclusion: β-冗余的作用是受约束语言下的结构数字完整性资源，可增强错误可见性和规范性检查，但不能取代经典错误控制编码中的显式冗余。

Abstract: Redundancy in non-standard numeration systems is often associated with robustness, but its practical value in finite digital arithmetic depends on how representation, storage, and repair are defined. We study intrinsic redundancy in finite beta-expansion systems using a bounded-window model that separates semantic non-uniqueness from canonical codebook admissibility. The model distinguishes arithmetic canonicalization from corruption repair and evaluates structural detectability, value-preserving repair of the observed state, and survival of the original value. For the golden-ratio system and related multinacci bases, we prove that a genuine single-digit corruption in a canonically injective finite codebook cannot be semantically recovered by exact repair without external information. Semantic survival under exact structural repair is possible only for localized multi-digit perturbations corresponding to algebraic rewrite identities, such as the equivalence of 100 and 011. Experiments comparing standard binary, signed-digit non-adjacent form, and multinacci systems quantify trade-offs among codebook sparsity, fault visibility, canonicalization cost, residual error, and boundary loss. The results show that intrinsic beta-redundancy is a constrained-language resource for structural digital integrity rather than a substitute for classical error-control redundancy.

</details>


### [96] [Wireless Decentralized Federated Learning via Device Clustering and Inter-Cluster Link Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08797)
*William Weijia Zheng,Hang Liu,Ying-Jun Angela Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出一种预算感知、以簇为中心的去中心化联邦学习框架，通过在关键滞后节点部署少量可靠回传链路，加速无线 D2D 网络中的模型聚合与收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统去中心化联邦学习依赖边缘设备间点对点模型交换，虽避免中心服务器并适合大规模 D2D 网络，但无线环境下多对多梯度共享会带来信道接入无序、通信误差、滞后节点影响和簇状网络中的共识变慢，导致整体收敛效率低。

Method: 方法先将网络划分为高内聚的设备簇，再在有限回传预算下选择簇头或滞后关键节点部署可靠回传链路，形成两层聚合协议：簇内进行快速并行模型聚合，簇间由簇头进行较低频率的信息交换。

Result: 理论上该框架可达到关于迭代次数 t 的 O(1/t) 收敛速率；在图像分类任务上的数值实验表明，相比已有 DFL 基线方法，仅使用少量策略性部署的回传链路即可显著加快收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在无线 DFL 中通过结合网络聚类与有限回传资源优化，可以有效缓解聚合瓶颈、提升网络连通性和模型共识速度，是一种兼顾通信预算与学习效率的可扩展方案。

Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (DFL) dispenses with the central server of classical FL by utilizing peer-to-peer model exchanges among edge devices. This server-free architecture enables ad-hoc, flexible distributed learning in large device-to-device (D2D) networks. However, wireless DFL converges slowly because peer-to-peer model aggregation incurs high delays and errors. Each DFL training round involves many-to-many gradient sharing over wireless channels, resulting in uncoordinated channel access, large communication errors from stragglers, and slow model consensus, especially in large-scale D2D networks with pronounced clustering structures. We address these aggregation bottlenecks by provisioning a few reliable backhaul links at straggling nodes to enhance network connectivity. Building on this idea, our budget-aware, cluster-centric DFL framework first partitions the network into densely connected clusters, and then allocates the limited backhaul budget to selected cluster heads. The resulting two-tier protocol executes fast, parallel model aggregation within clusters and infrequent inter-cluster exchanges among the heads, yielding an O(1/t) convergence rate in t iterations. Numerical experiments on image-classification tasks confirm that our approach accelerates convergence compared to state-of-the-art DFL baselines with only a few strategically placed backhaul links.

</details>


### [97] [Keyless Covert Communication Over Quantum MACs with General Message Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08898)
*Hassan ZivariFard,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究量子多址信道中的隐蔽经典通信，给出一般消息集合下的可达速率区域与若干容量结果，并通过有限维、经典高斯和单模玻色多址信道示例证明可实现正隐蔽速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐蔽通信与多址信道研究多集中于经典信道、特定消息结构或有限发射端场景；对于完全量子多址信道、任意数量发射端以及一般消息集合下能否实现正隐蔽速率与其速率区域，仍缺乏统一理论。

Method: 作者建立了完全量子多址信道下的一次性与渐近可达速率区域；针对经典-量子多址信道，在确定性编码限制下推导隐蔽容量；并将理论应用到带辅助者的有限维多址信道、经典高斯多址信道和单模玻色多址信道。

Result: 论文证明了在一般量子多址信道中实现正隐蔽速率是可行的；给出了经典-量子多址信道的一般消息集合隐蔽容量；结果涵盖并推广了若干已有经典多址通信、双发射端经典隐蔽通信和量子多址经典通信结果。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统展示了经典与量子多址信道中均可实现正速率隐蔽通信，并为多发射端、一般消息集合及物理信道模型下的隐蔽通信容量分析提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study covert classical communication over quantum multiple-access channels (MACs) with general message sets. Specifically, we consider a fully quantum MAC with arbitrary message sets and an arbitrary number of transmitters. We demonstrate the feasibility of achieving a positive covert rate over this channel and establish general one-shot and asymptotic achievable rate regions. For classical-quantum MACs with general message sets, we establish the covert capacity, when the transmitters are restricted to deterministic encoding. Our result recovers, as a special case, known results for classical communication over classical MACs with general message sets, covert communication of a classical message over a classical channel with two transmitters, and classical communication over quantum MACs. We provide three examples of MACs to which our results can be applied, either directly or indirectly, to achieve positive covert rates. Specifically, we first study covert communication over a finite-dimensional MAC with a helper. We then analyze a classical Gaussian MAC with a helper and derive its covert capacity. Finally, we extend the analysis to a single-mode bosonic MAC with a helper and show that positive covert rates can also be achieved in this setting. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to achieve positive-rate covert communication over both classical and quantum MACs.

</details>


### [98] [Study of Mixed-Integer Optimization Based on Graph-Based Decomposition for Cell-Free Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09017)
*Julio Cesar Cardoso Tesolin,Rodrigo C. de Lamare*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向用户中心无小区大规模多天线网络中混合离散-连续优化问题的RAN框架，并设计基于图的搜索-评估算法以提升能效优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在用户中心无小区大规模多天线网络中，服务簇选择等离散决策与资源分配等连续变量相互耦合，导致RAN级优化问题复杂、难以扩展。论文希望利用问题结构分解来提高搜索效率与可扩展性。

Method: 作者将可行服务状态空间抽象为具有汉明拓扑邻域的图，利用离散聚类决策与连续资源分配变量之间的结构分解，提出拓扑感知的搜索-评估流程，并设计图基搜索-评估（GBSE）算法，同时给出复杂度分析。

Result: 以RAN级能效最大化作为应用场景，数值结果表明，最小汉明邻域在可扩展性与探索能力之间具有较好折中，且GBSE算法优于现有技术。

Conclusion: 该框架为混合离散-连续RAN优化提供了有效的图建模与搜索机制，能够在保持较低复杂度的同时提升能效优化表现，适合大规模网络中的结构化优化任务。

Abstract: This letter develops a radio access network (RAN) framework for mixed discrete-continuous optimization problems that arise in user-centric cell=free massive multiple-antenna networks. The novel framework exploits the structural decomposition between discrete clustering decisions and continuous resource allocation variables by modeling the space of feasible serving states as a graph with Hamming-topology neighborhoods. A serving-state graph abstraction is introduced to enable topology-aware search-and-evaluate optimization procedures and a graph-based search-and-evaluate (GBSE) algorithm is devised along with their complexity analysis. Energy efficiency maximization at the RAN level is presented as an application of considered alongside the proposed framework and GBSE algorithm. Numerical results show that minimal Hamming neighborhoods offer an attractive trade-off between scalability and exploration capability in grap-based optimization and GBSE outperforms existing techniques.

</details>


### [99] [Continuous Aperture Array-Assisted Integrated Communication and Navigation in LEO Satellite Constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09030)
*Qi Wang,Xiaoming Chen,Qiao Qi,Zhaolin Wang,Yuanwei Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向低轨卫星星座的连续孔径阵列辅助通信导航一体化框架，通过联合波束成形在保证通信速率的同时提升导航定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星系统需要在共享频谱下同时支持高速通信与高精度导航；传统离散相控阵在空间自由度和性能上受限，而连续孔径阵列有潜力提升双功能系统性能，但其波束设计具有无限维优化难题。

Method: 构建基于电磁传播的多卫星协同传输模型，推导通信可达速率与导航Cramer-Rao下界；建立以最小化平均CRB为目标、受发射功率与最小速率约束的联合波束成形问题；引入ICAN信道子空间将连续孔径波束成形的无限维问题等价转化为有限维问题，并采用迭代凸优化算法求解。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提CAPA辅助波束成形算法相比传统离散相控阵结构及其他基准方案，在通信导航一体化性能上具有显著优势，尤其能降低导航CRB并维持通信服务质量。

Conclusion: 连续孔径阵列可有效增强低轨卫星通信导航一体化系统的空间处理能力；结合信道子空间降维与联合优化，可实现可计算且性能优越的双功能波束设计。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel continuous aperture array (CAPA)-assisted integrated communication and navigation (ICAN) framework for low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellations. Within this framework, an electromagnetic-based collaborative transmission model is developed, in which multiple satellites equipped with CAPAs simultaneously radiate downlink data streams and navigation reference signals over shared spectrum. Building upon this, the achievable communication rate and the navigation Cramer-Rao bound (CRB) are derived, which explicitly characterize the intrinsic coupling between the dual-function beamformers and system performance. To improve the positioning accuracy with communication quality of service guarantee, a joint beamforming optimization problem is formulated to minimize the average CRB subject to transmit power budgets and minimum rate constraints. To tackle the inherent infinite-dimensionality of the CAPA beamformer design, an ICAN channel subspace is introduced to equivalently transform the formulation into a tractable finite-dimensional problem, which is then efficiently solved via an iterative convex optimization algorithm. Finally, numerical results demonstrate that the proposed CAPA-assisted beamforming design algorithm significantly outperforms conventional discrete phased array architectures and other benchmark schemes, yielding notable improvements in ICAN performance.

</details>


### [100] [Modeling and Analysis for Multiple-Layer LEO Satellite Internet of Things Constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09035)
*Ming Ying,Xiaoming Chen,Qiao Qi,Yichao Xu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于随机几何的多层低轨卫星物联网星座建模与分析框架，将Rician信道和Cox点过程结合，并推导关键性能指标的闭式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层低轨卫星物联网星座可提供全球多星覆盖，但在实际Rician衰落卫星信道下，其性能分析因理论建模复杂、数学表达难处理而尚不清楚。

Method: 作者构建随机几何分析框架，使用Cox点过程描述多层LEO卫星星座分布，并引入新的Rician信道近似方法以处理难以解析的表达式；进一步推导连接概率、覆盖概率和短包传输速率等指标的精确闭式表达。

Result: 大量仿真验证了所提模型的准确性和有效性，并揭示了多层LEO卫星IoT星座部署与优化中的重要设计规律。

Conclusion: 该研究为LEO卫星物联网星座在Rician信道下的建模与性能分析提供了新的理论工具，同时可为实际系统部署和参数优化提供指导。

Abstract: To provide multiple-satellite coverage for global Internet of Things (IoT), a low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite IoT constellation usually contains multiple-layer orbits with different altitudes. However, the performance of multiple-layer LEO satellite IoT constellations under practical Rician fading satellite channels remains unknown due to complex theoretical modeling and intractable mathematical analysis. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a stochastic geometry-based modeling and analysis framework for multiple-layer LEO satellite IoT constellations, integrating Rician channel modeling and Cox point processes. Specifically, we introduce a novel channel approximation method to overcome the intractable expressions caused by the Rician fading. Building on this method, we derive exact closed-form expressions for key performance metrics, including connectivity probability, coverage probability, and transmission rate, especially in the case of IoT short-packet transmission. Extensive simulation results validate the accuracy and effectiveness of the proposed model and reveal significant design insights. The results not only provide new theoretical perspectives for modeling and analysis of LEO satellite IoT constellations but also offer practical guidance for system deployment and optimization.

</details>


### [101] [Two-dimensional constacyclic codes over finite chain rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09117)
*Vaishali Singh,Sucheta Dutt,Ridhima Thakral*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限链环上二维(λ,μ)-常循环码的代数结构，并给出其生成元刻画及满足MHDR性质的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维常循环码在有限环编码理论中具有重要代数结构与距离性质；在有限链环及给定剩余域条件下，系统刻画其结构和最优距离条件具有理论意义。

Method: 通过分析有限链环中的本原幂等元，分解相关代数结构，并据此确定二维(λ,μ)-常循环码的生成元；进一步研究相对于秩的最大汉明距离性质。

Result: 论文获得了二维(λ,μ)-常循环码的结构描述，并给出了这类码在有限链环上成为MHDR码的条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为有限链环上二维常循环码提供了明确的代数结构刻画，并建立了其最优距离性质的判定依据。

Abstract: The main focus of this paper is on the algebraic structure of two-dimensional $(λ,μ)$-constacyclic codes of length $\ell\mathrm{m}$ over finite chain rings with residue field $\mathbb{F}_q$, where $q \equiv 1 \pmod{r\mathrm{m}}$ and $r$ denotes the multiplicative order of $\barμ$. In this paper, the structure of two-dimensional $(λ,μ)$-constacyclic codes is obtained. Our approach relies on analysing primitive idempotents within the finite chain ring to determine the generators of these codes. We also find the condition under which two-dimensional constacyclic codes are maximum Hamming distance with respect to rank (MHDR) over finite chain rings.

</details>


### [102] [Generative Communications: Overview, Technologies, and Trends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09183)
*Wenjun Zhang,Zhiyong Chen,Tong Wu,Guo Lu,Li Song,Feng Yang,Meixia Tao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式通信（GenCom）作为面向6G的新型通信范式：由大规模AI模型驱动语义理解、推理与内容生成，以最小充分信息传输替代传统比特级精确复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通信系统主要追求比特准确传输，难以充分利用生成式AI在语义理解、推理和内容合成方面的能力。随着图像、视频等内容生成能力快速提升，6G网络需要一种更高效、更智能、面向语义和生成的新通信机制。

Method: 文章形式化定义GenCom概念，阐明其AI原生与生成驱动特性；提出核心机制，即发送端仅传递最小且足够的信息，接收端利用共享生成先验和知识库合成目标输出；进一步设计两层GenCom架构，并结合关键使能技术进行说明。

Result: 通过四类代表性应用场景分析，文章表明GenCom可实现超高效传输、语义层鲁棒性，并支持新的网络功能。

Conclusion: GenCom将通信重新定义为受控生成而非数据复现，为6G网络提供了有前景的发展路径；未来仍需在基础理论、实时处理等方面开展深入研究。

Abstract: The groundbreaking development of generative artificial intelligence (AI) is rapidly boosting the ability to generate content such as images and videos, reshaping communication paradigms. This article introduces generative communications (GenCom), a novel paradigm for 6G networks in which large AI models (LAMs) drive semantic understanding, reasoning, and content generation, embedding these into the communication process. Unlike traditional systems that strictly pursue accurate bit transmission, GenCom enables transmitters to convey only minimal yet sufficient information, while receivers leverage shared generative priors and knowledge bases to synthesize the intended output. Communication is thus redefined as controlled generation rather than data reproduction. We formalize the concept of GenCom, clarify its AI-native and generation-driven properties, and present its core mechanisms. A two-layer GenCom architecture supported by key enabling technologies is proposed, and analysis of four representative application scenarios demonstrates that GenCom offers ultra-efficient transmission, semantic-level robustness, and new network functions. Finally, we outline future research directions, including foundational theory and real-time processing, highlighting a promising pathway toward 6G networks.

</details>


### [103] [Low-Complexity Successive-Cancellation List Decoding of $2\times2$ Kernel Non-Binary Polar Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09257)
*Xinyu Zhou,Pingping Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对2×2核非二元极化码的NB-SCL译码复杂度过高问题，提出三种低复杂度列表译码方案，在接近传统NB-SCL误帧率性能的同时显著降低路径扩展、排序与剪枝开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非二元连续消除列表译码在每个信息符号处会将每条保留路径扩展为q个候选分支，导致路径分裂数量大、排序复杂度高、硬件/计算实现代价较高，因此需要在尽量保持FER性能的前提下降低译码复杂度。

Method: 论文首先提出SR-NBSCL译码器：当当前符号可靠性足够高时跳过路径分裂。随后利用最终Rate-1节点结构，将最后一组信息符号切换为简化NB-SC译码，形成ESR-NBSCL。进一步地，论文引入累积可靠性偏差（ARD）度量，在不可靠符号处进行自适应分支预剪枝，提出ABP-NBSCL，以在排序前减少候选分支数并降低主导排序复杂度。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提译码器的FER性能接近传统NB-SCL，但复杂度显著降低。其中ABP-NBSCL在多个测试SNR下将路径分裂数量降低超过80%，且性能损失可以忽略。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过可靠性驱动的跳过分裂、Rate-1节点简化译码以及基于ARD的自适应分支预剪枝，可以有效降低2×2核NBPC列表译码复杂度，同时基本保持传统NB-SCL的纠错性能。

Abstract: Non-binary successive cancellation list (NB-SCL) decoding expands each surviving path into $q$ candidate branches at every information symbol, which causes high path expansion, sorting, and pruning complexity. To address this issue, this paper proposes low-complexity list decoding algorithms for $2\times2$ kernel non-binary polar codes (NBPCs). First, we design a split-reduced non-binary successive cancellation list (SR-NBSCL) decoder that skips path splitting when the current symbol is sufficiently reliable. We then exploit the final Rate-1 node structure and switch the last group of information symbols to simplified non-binary successive cancellation (NB-SC) decoding, resulting in the enhanced split-reduced non-binary successive cancellation list (ESR-NBSCL) decoder. To further reduce branch expansion at unreliable symbols, we introduce an accumulated reliability-deviation (ARD) metric and propose an adaptive branch-pruning non-binary successive cancellation list (ABP-NBSCL) decoder, which prunes unreliable candidate branches before sorting and then reduces the dominant sorting complexity. Simulation results show that the proposed decoders achieve frame-error-rate (FER) performance close to that of conventional NB-SCL decoding with much lower complexity. In particular, the ABP-NBSCL decoder reduces the path splitting number (PSN) by more than $80\%$ at several tested signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs), with a negligible performance loss.

</details>


### [104] [On the Gaussian-Quadratic Rate-Distortion Function for Vector Sources with Individual Distortion Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09545)
*Shuao Chen,Junyuan Gao,Yuxuan Shi,Yongpeng Wu,Giuseppe Caire,H. Vincent Poor,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究任意长度高斯源在逐分量二次失真约束下的有损压缩率失真函数，给出超出传统 Hadamard 下界紧性条件的系统解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Hadamard 不等式给出的率失真下界仅在半定条件（SDC）成立时紧；当 SDC 不成立时，下界松弛且缺乏解析解。同时，源相关性如何定量影响 RDF 的基础关系尚不完整。

Method: 作者从一般协方差和失真约束出发，分析最优重构源的谱性质并强化 SDC 的标量不等式形式；提出层级相关协方差族，并证明二类型相关（2-TC）模型可作为其解析基础；进一步在 2-TC 协方差与二类型失真（2-TD）约束下，对失真平面划分区域并推导完整 RDF 与最优失真/速率分配。

Result: 论文在 SDC 成立时得到了显式包含源相关性的 RDF，并从失真约束和相关性角度刻画 SDC；在 2-TC/2-TD 设置下建立了七个区域上的完整 RDF，明确各区域的最优分配；在各向同性相关与相同约束下给出了逐分量压缩率，显示利用相关性可显著降低压缩成本。

Conclusion: 该工作补足了高斯二次率失真理论中 SDC 不充分情形与相关性作用机制的解析空缺，表明完整 RDF 的核心在于刻画最优失真之间的相关结构。

Abstract: This paper investigates the Gaussian-quadratic lossy compression with arbitrary source length under individual distortion constraints. The rate-distortion function (RDF) is lower-bounded by a Hadamard inequality-based rate, which is tight if and only if the semidefinite condition (SDC) holds. Otherwise, this bound becomes loose, and analytical results are lacking. Moreover, the fundamental quantitative relationship between source correlations and the RDF remains incomplete. In this paper, we provide new theoretical results under different source covariance matrices and distortion constraints. First, under arbitrary covariance and distortion constraints, we obtain the spectral properties of the optimal source reconstruction achieving the RDF, and a stronger scalar inequality version of the SDC. We propose a class of source covariance matrices based on hierarchical correlations and show that studying the two-type correlation (2-TC) model is sufficient to establish the analytical foundation for the broader class. Under this covariance, we obtain the RDF with source correlations explicitly incorporated when the SDC holds, and analyze the SDC from the perspectives of distortion constraints and source correlations. Next, under the 2-TC covariance and two-type distortion (2-TD) constraints, we establish the complete RDFs over seven regions on a distortion plane, with the optimal distortion (rate) allocations determined in each region. It is revealed that the essence of pursuing the complete RDF lies in thoroughly analyzing the correlations between the optimal distortions. Finally, under isotropic correlation and identical constraints, we provide the per-component compression rate and show that exploiting correlations can significantly reduce compression costs.

</details>


### [105] [Density Evolution of Soft-Decision Collapsed Projection-Aggregation Decoding for Reed-Muller Codes over the BIAWGN Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09602)
*Jiajie Li,Marvin Rübenacke,Warren J. Gross*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文分析RM码在BIAWGN信道下CPA译码软输出的密度演化，证明其软判决输出为精确边缘概率且具有对称性，并提出可解释CPA快速收敛的近似密度演化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RM码已被证明在多类信道上可达容量，而PA/CPA译码在实验中表现出接近最大似然译码的性能；因此需要从理论上解释CPA译码的软信息演化、译码机制与快速收敛原因。

Method: 作者研究CPA译码软输出的密度函数，证明软判决CPA输出精确边缘概率及对称性；据此构建密度演化模型，并用硬判决近似投影步骤和快速Hadamard变换译码以简化分析，随后通过BIAWGN信道仿真与渐近分析验证模型。

Result: 仿真显示所提密度演化模型能够定性捕捉CPA译码返回软信息均值和方差的快速下降过程，解释CPA译码的快速收敛；渐近分析表明，在码率趋于零的RM码序列上，CPA译码可实现错误概率趋于零。

Conclusion: 该工作为CPA译码提供了理论密度演化框架，揭示其软信息收敛机制，并证明在低码率渐近场景下CPA译码具有可靠译码潜力。

Abstract: Reed-Muller (RM) codes have been shown to achieve capacity over a range of channels, and recently proposed projection-aggregation (PA) decoding has been experimentally shown to achieve near-maximum-likelihood decoding performance. These recent achievements motivate theoretical research on PA decoding. In this work, we analyze the density function of the soft output from collapsed projection-aggregation (CPA) decoding for RM codes over the binary-input additive white Gaussian noise (BIAWGN) channel. We prove that soft-decision CPA decoding returns an exact marginal probability and is symmetric. Based on the analysis, we build a density evolution model for CPA decoding. To simplify the density evolution, we approximate the projection and the fast Hadamard transform decoding using hard-decision decoding. Simulation results over the BIAWGN channel show that our proposed density evolution model captures the fast reduction in the mean and the variance of the soft information returned from the CPA decoding, which qualitatively explains the decoding mechanism and the fast convergence speed of the CPA decoding. We perform an asymptotic analysis based on the proposed density evolution, and we show that CPA decoding can achieve a vanishing error probability for RM codes with a vanishing code rate.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Interval Certifications for Multilayered Perceptrons via Lattice Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08773)
*Merkouris Papamichail,Konstantinos Varsos,Giorgos Flouris,João Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将对抗鲁棒性认证形式化为格遍历问题，统一研究可靠认证与完备认证，并给出算法、复杂性结果和系统验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗鲁棒性是 AI 安全中的基础问题。现有研究主要关注在输入邻域内预测不变的可靠认证，而较少研究刻画一旦离开某个区域预测必然改变的完备认证。论文希望建立一个更统一、更严格的理论框架来理解这些认证问题。

Method: 作者将包含输入点的轴对齐超矩形区间组织为一个格结构，把 MLP 分类器的认证问题归约为格遍历问题；提出格遍历算子，并结合 refine-and-verify 迭代流程，调用形式化 MLP 验证器来保证可靠认证的极大性与完备认证的极小性。同时分析相关目标优化问题，包括一般区间和对称区间中的算法复杂度。

Result: 论文表明，完备认证的最小解可通过多项式次数的 oracle 调用获得；相对地，可靠认证不存在类似性质，并被证明具有强不可处理性。对于对称区间，即 l_infty 球，作者给出了对数级算法。此外，论文实现并评估了 ParallelepipedoNN 系统。

Conclusion: 该工作为 MLP 对抗鲁棒性认证提供了格论视角下的统一理论框架，首次系统引入并研究完备认证，揭示了可靠认证与完备认证在优化复杂性上的不对称性，并通过原型系统验证了方法的可行性。

Abstract: In this work we present a rigorous theoretical framework to a foundational problem of AI safety, namely adversarial robustness. In particular, we show that the adversarial robustness problem can be reduced to a lattice traversal problem. Each element of this lattice corresponds to an interval, i.e., an axis-aligned hyper-rectangle, containing an input point $\mathbf{x}$. Consider a multilayered perceptron classifier (MLP). An interval $I$ constitutes a sound certification if $\mathbf{x} \in I$ and $\mathbf{x}$ can be freely perturbed in $I$ without changing the MLP's prediction. Complementarily, an interval $I$ constitutes a complete certification if $\mathbf{x} \in I$ and when $\mathbf{x}$ moves outside of $I$ the MLP's prediction is guaranteed to change. While the sound certification problem corresponds to the well-studied adversarial robustness, complete certifications have not been examined in the literature. We develop lattice traversal operators, which we apply in a refine & verify iterative scheme. Using formal MLP verifiers, sound maximality and complete minimality are guaranteed. Moreover, we examine objective optimization problems. There we discover some interesting asymmetries. For complete certifications, the minimum solution is obtained in polynomial oracle calls. This does not hold for sound certifications, where we prove strong intractability results. Additionally, we examine optimization problems in symmetric intervals (i.e., $\ell_\infty$-spheres), where we provide logarithmic algorithms. Finally, we present an empirical evaluation, using the novel ParallelepipedoNN system.

</details>


### [107] [CogniConsole: Externalizing Inference-Time Control as a Formal Abstraction for Reliable LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08774)
*Vanessa Figueiredo,Wilter Franceschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张LLM系统可靠性不仅取决于模型能力，还显著受推理时控制层影响；提出CogniConsole架构，并通过探针实验表明结构化脚手架可降低输出方差和失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将LLM可靠性问题归因于模型能力不足或规模不够。作者希望挑战这一单一视角，指出任务框定、上下文选择与约束协调等推理时控制机制同样是可靠性的关键来源。

Method: 提出CogniConsole，将推理时控制外部化为结构化接口，结合程序化协调与有边界的提示式推理。在多步骤交互环境中设计489个面向可控性的探针，对比从非结构化到完全脚手架化的不同控制条件，并在固定模型架构下评估输出方差与失败率。

Result: 随着结构化脚手架程度提高，系统输出方差和失败率呈系统性下降。实验显示，在模型架构不变的情况下，增强推理时控制即可改善可靠性。

Conclusion: 许多LLM失效模式，如上下文漂移和约束遵循不一致，并非单纯源于能力不足，而是来自控制规范不足。推理时控制应被视为LLM系统设计与评估中的一等抽象，未来可靠性提升不应仅依赖模型扩展。

Abstract: Reliability in large language model (LLM) systems is typically framed as a function of model capability. We challenge this by demonstrating that reliability is significantly influenced by \emph{inference-time control} -- the computational layer governing task framing and context selection. We introduce \emph{CogniConsole}, an architectural instantiation that externalizes this control into a structured interface combining programmatic coordination with bounded prompt-based reasoning. Through \emph{controllability-oriented probes} ($N=489$) in a multi-step interactive environment, we show that increasing structural scaffolding -- from unstructured to fully scaffolded -- \textbf{systematically reduces output variance and failure rates under a fixed model architecture}. Our results indicate that many observed failure modes, such as context drift and inconsistent constraint adherence, arise from under-specified control rather than insufficient capability. This work provides an empirical basis for treating inference-time control as a first-class abstraction, opening new directions for designing and evaluating LLM systems beyond scaling alone.

</details>


### [108] [GATS: Graph-Augmented Tree Search with Layered World Models for Efficient Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08894)
*Maureese Williams,Dymitr Nowicki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GATS 通过图增强树搜索与分层世界模型，在规划推理阶段不调用 LLM，仍实现更高成功率、确定性输出和更低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体规划方法（如 LATS、ReAct）在多步规划中高度依赖 LLM 推理，导致计算开销大、结果随机性强，且在长视野、多分支和死胡同场景中表现不稳定。

Method: 提出 Graph-Augmented Tree Search（GATS），结合基于 UCB1 的系统化树搜索与三层世界模型：L1 精确符号动作匹配，L2 从执行日志学习统计规律，L3 对未知动作使用 LLM 预测；训练或构建后在规划推理阶段不再调用 LLM。

Result: 在含分支路径和死胡同的合成规划任务上，GATS 成功率为 100%，优于 LATS 的 92% 和 ReAct 的 64%；在覆盖 12 类压力场景的测试中，GATS 仍为 100%，LATS 为 88.9%，ReAct 为 23.9%。GATS 每任务规划阶段 LLM 调用为 0，LATS 平均 37 次，且 GATS 多次运行零方差。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合学习型世界模型的系统化搜索可在智能体规划中显著优于依赖 LLM 引导探索的方法，尤其在成本、稳定性和长程规划可靠性方面具有优势。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown promise in multi-step planning tasks, but existing approaches like LATS (Language Agent Tree Search) and ReAct rely heavily on LLM inference during planning, leading to high computational costs and stochastic behavior. We present \textbf{GATS} (Graph-Augmented Tree Search), a planning framework that combines systematic UCB1-based tree search with a layered world model to eliminate LLM calls during inference while achieving superior planning performance. Our three-layer world model integrates: (L1) exact symbolic action matching, (L2) statistics learned from execution logs, and (L3) LLM-based prediction for unknown actions. On synthetic planning tasks with branching paths and dead-ends, GATS achieves \textbf{100\% success rate} compared to 92 % for LATS and 64\% for ReAct. On a comprehensive stress test spanning 12 challenging scenarios -- including coding workflows, web navigation, and long-horizon tasks -- GATS maintains \textbf{100\% success} while LATS drops to 88.9 % and ReAct to 23.9%. GATS requires \textbf{zero LLM calls per task} during planning (vs. 37 per task for LATS) and produces deterministic plans with zero variance across runs. Our results demonstrate that systematic search with learned world models can substantially outperform LLM-guided exploration for agent planning.

</details>


### [109] [Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench: Testing the Limits of Agents on Long-Horizon Terminal Tasks with Dense Reward-Based Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08964)
*Zongxia Li,Zhongzhi Li,Yucheng Shi,Ruhan Wang,Junyao Yang,Zhichao Liu,Xiyang Wu,Anhao Li,Yue Yu,Ninghao Liu,Lichao Sun,Haotao Mi,LeoweiLiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench，一个面向长时程终端智能体的基准，包含46个跨9类的复杂任务，并通过细粒度子任务提供密集奖励与部分得分；实验显示当前前沿模型在该基准上成功率较低，长时程规划、上下文管理和迭代调试仍有显著提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端基准多关注短时间、定义明确且仅依据最终结果评分的简单任务，导致奖励信号稀疏，无法充分衡量智能体在开放式、长流程任务中的中间进展与部分解决能力。
